## 21
1 Á̤-Hó̤-Hua bíng sá̤ⁿ-dih sê̤ gô̤ng ē uā, ka̍ng-go̍ Sah-la̍h, iā bíng sê̤ i̍ng-hṳ̂ ē si keoh i.
2 A̍-beh-la̍h-hâng néng lô̤ sí-cā̤. Sah-la̍h dua̍-sing, ga̍u Siō̤ng-Da̤̍ sê̤ gô̤ng di̍h-gí, do̍i A̍-beh-la̍h-hâng sa̤ⁿ seo̍h gá̤ giô̤ⁿ.
3 Sah-la̍h do̍i A̍-beh-la̍h-hâng sê̤ saⁿ ē giô̤ⁿ, A̍-beh la̍h-hâng heō i ē giô̤ⁿ miá ga̤̍u Î-sah.
4 Gā ū bā̤ di̍h, A̍-beh-la̍h-hâng cuh bíng Siō̤ng-Da̤̍ ē mīng. ta̤̍ giô̤ⁿ Î-sah héng gah-lâ̤.
5 Dng i giô̤ⁿ Î-sah cheoh-si̍ sí-cā̤, A̍-beh-la̍h-hâng dû-dû seo̍h-bā ho̍i.
6 Sah-la̍h gô̤ng, Siō̤ng-Da̤̍ sâi guâ huaⁿ-hî ā̤ cha̤̍u; go̤h-náng tiaⁿ-mói, iā beoh gah guâ cá̤ huaⁿ-hî cha̤̍u.
7 lô̤, guâ do̍i i saⁿ ā giô̤ⁿ; sá̤ⁿ-di̍h hn̍g-náng ā̤ ṳ̄-cô̤ gah A̍-beh-la̍h-hâng gô̤ng, Sah-la̍h beoh saⁿ giô̤ⁿ lē?2
8 Ca̤̍ giô̤ⁿ ciāng-ciāng diô̤ng-duā, cuh dē̤ⁿ-neng. Î-sah dē̤-neng hṳ̂ seo̍h di̍h, A̍-beh-la̍h-hâng bāng cīng hā̤u ē
9 ciû-si̍h. Ai-gi̍h ē a-táu Hā-gah do̍i A̍-beh- -hâng sê̤ saⁿ ē giô̤ⁿ pî-sa̤̍u, keoi h-la̍h kua̍ⁿ-gi̍ng da̤u̍h.
10 Go̍-eô i gah A̍-beh-la̍h-hâng gô̤ng: a̤u̍h de̤̍h ca̤̍ a-táu, léng i giô̤. ng ê-táu ē giô̤ⁿ n̄g-tang gah gug ̍iô̤ⁿ Î-sah cá̤ deh gi-gia̍h.
11 A̍-beh-la̍h-hâng ing giô̤ⁿ ē é̤ng-go̍, La̤h ca̤̍ seō duā gua̍-huái.2
12 iō̤ng-Da̤̍ gah A̍-beh-la̍h-hâng gô̤ng, Dṳ̂ n̄g-sâi ing dṳ̂ giô̤ⁿ léng a-táu cuh gua̍-huái; tang tiaⁿ Sah-la̍h sê̤ gô̤ng; ing iú Î-sah sê̤ cheo̍ ē cia̍ⁿ ā̤ ching ceo̍ dṳ̂ ē hā̤u-a̤̍.
13 Nō̤ ca̤̍ a-táu ē giô̤ⁿ, Guâ iā beo sâi i hā̤u-a̤̍ siáⁿ ceo̍ duā co̤̍h, ing iā sī dṳ̂ sê̤ saⁿ ē.
14 A̍-beh-la̍h. hâng cô̤-cô̤ bā-kî, ta̤̍h biâⁿ gah seo̍h pói-dē̤ ē cûi, keoh Hā-gah iāⁿ a̤ⁿ-táu lē, sâi dua̍ i ē giô̤ⁿ giáⁿ Aṳ̍; Hā-gah cuh kî-sing ga̍u Be̍h-sī-ba ē ko̤̍ng-iâ, giáⁿ lí giáⁿ kṳ̍ n̄g-cai dō.
15 Seo̍h pói-dē̤ ē cûi sâi é̤ng, cuh ba̍ng i giô̤ⁿ leo̍h seo̍h dau sa̤̍-dau chiu̍ ō̤-dâ̤.
16 Cuh lī kui o̤h-lio̤̍h ū sa̤u̍h-ci̍ng ē dā̤ hiô̤ hōiⁿ; gah i du̍i-mi̍ng sē̤, gô̤ng, Guâ bā̤-siâ-dih kua̍ⁿ ca̤̍ giô̤ⁿ sî kṳ̍; cuh sē̤ du̍i-mi̍ng, duā-siaⁿ hâu.
17 Siō̤ng-Da̤̍ tiaⁿ-mói da̍i-giô̤ⁿ ē siaⁿ-ing; Siō̤ng-Da̤̍ ē seo̍-ciâ iú ting lē huā Hā-gah, gô̤ng, Hā-gah, seo̍h-moi dāi-li̍ lē? Dṳ̂ n̄g-sâi giaⁿ; dṳ̂ giô̤ⁿ deó deoh-seo̍h ūi-chṳ̍, Siō̤ng-Da̤̍ i-ging tiaⁿ-mói i ē siaⁿ-ing lâ̤u.
18 Dṳ̂ bā-kî, keng dṳ̂ ē giô̤ⁿ cuh hû-iô̤ng i; Guâ beoh sâi i ē hā̤u-a̤̍ siáⁿ ceo̍ duā co̤̍h.
19 Siō̤ng-Da̤̍ sâi Hā-gah ma̍h-ciu gng, kua̍ⁿ-gi̍ng ū ûi-câⁿ; cuh kṳ̍ dâ̤u seo̍h dē̤ cûi mua-muâ leoh di̍i-giô̤ⁿ sia̍h.
20 Sō̤ng-Da̤̍ bô̤-hō ca̤̍ da̍i-giô̤ⁿ; i iô̤ng-duā, do̍ leo̍h ko̤̍ng-iâ, duā sa-lō leo̍h su̍h-ci̍ng.
21 I dô Ba-láng ko̤̍ng-iá; i ná̤u-lâ̤ ta̤̍ i teô Ai-gi̍h ē sîng-ná̤u-giô̤ⁿ ceo̍ lāu-mâ.
22 Do̤ng-sí A̍-bi̍-bî-leh léng i e cio̤ng-geong Hi-go̤h gah A̍-beh-la̍h-hâng gô̤ng. Háng dṳ̂ sê̤ ceo̍ seō, deo mó̤ng Siō̤ng-Da̤̍ bô̤-hō.
23 Dṳ̂ da leo̍h cî-ciâ, da̤u̍h cī Siō̤ng-Da̤̍ gah guâ huah-sā̤. n̄g-tang hio̤̍ng guâ léng guâ giô̤ⁿ gah seong ū ha̤u-sing: guâ ū seh-na hā̤u-dāi dṳ̂ dṳ̂ iā da̤u̍h ah-na hā̤u-dāi guâ, léng dṳ̂ sê̤ cāng-sí do̍ ē dā̤-ho̤ng.
24 A̍-beh-la̍h-hâng gô̤ng. Guâ kêng huah-sā̤.
25 Sá̤ⁿ. di̍h A̍-bi̍-bî-leh ē nó-cái nga̤, cia̍ng A̍-beh-la̍h-hâng ē câⁿ: da A̍-beh-la̍h-hâng ing-ūi ca̤̍ seō ceh-bī A̍-bi̍-bî-leh.
26 A̍-bi̍-bî-leh gô̤ng. Hn̍g-náng ceo̍ ca̤̍ seō, guâ n̄g-cai; dṳ̂ iā bōi-céng gah guâ gô̤ng. ging-nua̍ guâ cia̍ⁿ tiaⁿ-mói ca̤̍ seō.
27 A̍-beh-la̍h-hâng ta̤̍h gú á̤uⁿ. sa̍ng keoh A̍-bi̍-bí-leh: nn̄g gá̤ náng cuh cá̤ li̍h-io̤h.
28 A̍-beh-la̍h-hâng buh ta̤̍h chih táu beô á̤uⁿ-giô̤ⁿ, ba̍ng leo̍h seo̍h ūi-chṳ̍.
29 A̍-bi̍-bî-leh gah A̍-beh-la̍h-hâng gô̤ng, Chih táu á̤uⁿ-giô̤ⁿ, dṳ̂ līng-gōi ba̍ng leo̍h seo̍h ūi-chṳ̍, sī seo̍h-moi i̍-seo̍ lē?
30 A̍-beh-la̍h-hâng ng gô̤ng, Dṳ̂ dih-dih iú guâ chiû ē siu cî chih táu á̤uⁿ-giô̤ⁿ. ta̤̍ guâ eeo̍ geo̍h ca̤̍ câⁿ ē bíng-gṳ̍.
31 Ing n̄g gá̤ náng cá̤ leo̍h hiô̤ huah-sā̤, go̍-cheô miá ca̤̍ ūi-chṳ̍ ga̤̍u leō se̍h-sī-ba.
32 Nn̄g gá̤, náng deó Se̍h-sī-ba li̍h-io̤h āu. A̍-bi̍-bi-leh cuh gah i cio̤ng-geong Hi-go̤h, kî-sing hói-táu kṳ̍ Hi-lī-seō ē dā̤-ho̤ng.
33 A̍-beh-la̍h-hâng cai chiu̍ leó Be̍h-sī-ba, leo̍h hṳ̂-hiô̤ dô̤-go̤̍ îng-seng Siō̤ng-Da̤̍ Á̤-Hó̤-Hua miá.
34 A̍-beh-la̍h-hâng leo̍h Hi- lī-seō ē dā̤ cāng-do̍ chu̍i gúî.
## 22
1 Ca̤̍ seō í-hā̤u, Siō̤ng-Da̤̍ sih-po̤̍ A̍-beh-la̍h-hâng, ga̤̍u i gô̤ng, A̍-beh-la̍h-hâng ā. I i̍ng gô̤ng, Guâ deó cî-ciâ.
2 Siō̤ng-Da̤̍ gô̤ng, Da cio̤ng dṳ̂ sê̤ sā̤u ē do̤̍h-ceô Î-sah, kṳ̍ Mó̤-lī-a̍ ē dā̤; Guâ beoh cî-sī seo̍h gá̤ suaⁿ, deó hiô̤ he̤̍ng i ceo̍ hôi-sa̤u-ca̤̍.
3 A̍-beh-la̍h-hâng cô̤-cô̤ bā-kî, ṳ̄-bī ā lṳ́, dua̍ nn̄g gá̤ nó-cái, léng i giô̤ⁿ Î-sah; ing-ūi hôi-sa̤u-ca̤̍ pua̍ chó̤, kî-sing kṳ̍ Siō̤ng-Da̤̍ sê̤ cî-sī ē ūi-chṳ̍.
4 Ga̍u dā̤-so̤ⁿ di̍h, A̍-beh-la̍h-hâng gio̤̍h-kî ma̍h-ciu, hōiⁿ-hōiⁿ kua̍ⁿ hiô̤ ūi-chṳ̍.
5 Cuh gah nó-cái gô̤ng. Dṳ̂ gah lṳ́ meô deó cî-ciâ dêng; guâ gah da̍i-giô̤ⁿ kṳ̍ hṳ̂-hiô̤ lâ̤-ba̍i lō̤ cuh hói-táu.
6 A̍-beh-la̍h-hâng sâi Î-sah guāⁿ hôi-sa̤u-ca̤̍ ē chó̤, ga̍i chiû lē ta̤̍h hôi gah deo; nn̄g gá̤ náng cá̤ giáⁿ.
7 Î-sah gah ló̤-bō̤ A̍-beh-la̍h-hâng gô̤ng, Guâ ló̤-bō̤ ā. A̍-beh-la̍h-hâng i̍ng gô̤ng, Guâ giô̤ⁿ, guâ deó cî-ciâ. Î-sah gô̤ng, Hôi gah chó̤ deo cé̤ⁿ-bī lâ̤u; nō̤ ceo̍ hôi-sa̤u-ca̤̍ ē á̤uⁿ-giô̤ⁿ deó deoh-leo lē?
8 A̍-beh-la̍h-hâng gô̤ng, Guâ giô̤ⁿ ā, Siō̤ng-Da̤̍ dih-dih ga̍i ṳ̄-bī á̤uⁿ-giô̤ⁿ ceo̍ hôi-sa̤u-ca̤̍. Bíng ah-na, nn̄g gá̤ náng cá̤ giáⁿ.
9 Ga̍u Siō̤ng-Da̤̍ sê̤ cî-sī ē ūi-chṳ̍, A̍-beh-la̍h-hâng deó hṳ̂-hiô̤ de̤h ca̤̍-dái, bá̤ ca̤̍ chó̤, bo̤̍h i giô̤ⁿ Î-sah, cio̤ng i giô̤ⁿ ba̍ng leo̍h ca̤̍-dái ē chó̤ mi̍ng-dêng.
10 A̍-beh-la̍h-hâng cheong chiû ta̤̍h deo, beoh tái i giô̤ⁿ.
11 Á̤-Hó̤-Huu ē seo̍-ciâ iú ting lē huā gô̤ng, A̍-beh-la̍h-hâng, A̍-beh-la̍h-hâng. I i̍ng gô̤ng, Guâ deó cî-ciâ.
12 Seo̍-ciâ gô̤ng. Dṳ̂ n̄g-tang hō̤-chiû tái Sa̤̍-Gi̍.u: â. ca̤̍ lai-gio̤ⁿ, deo nn̄g-tang hāi i, dṳ̂ beó a̍i-sih do̤̍h-ceô, ā̤ kêng cio̤ng i he̤̍ng keoh Guâ.
13 A̍-beh-la̍h-hâng gio̤̍h-kî ma̍h-ciu a̤̍, kua̍ⁿ-gi̍ng āu-li̍ ū gang ē míng-á̤uⁿ, gah déng leo̍h chiu̍-pa̍ng: A̍-beh-la̍h-hâng cuh kṳ̍ dia̍h ca̤̍ á̤uⁿ, ta̤̍ giô̤ⁿ he̤̍ng ceo̍ hôi-sa̤u-ca̤̍.
14 A̍-beh-la̍h-hâng ching ca̤̍ dā̤-ho̤ng miá ga̤̍u Á̤-Hó̤-Hua Ih-le̍h; ga̍u da ho̤h ū náng gô̤ng, Deó Á̤-Hó̤-Hua ē suaⁿ, dih-dih ā̤ kua̍ⁿ-gi̍ng.
15 A̍-Hó-Hua ē seo̍-ciâ. iú ting lē dā̤-dī hói ga̤̍u A̍-beh-la̍h-hâng, gông,
16 Á-Hó-Hua gông, Dṳ̂ gi̍-céng ceo̍ ca̤̍ seō, beó a̍i-sih dṳ̂ ga̍i cî beông-sing huah-sā̤,
17 dih-dih seo̍ duā ho̤h-ki̍ keoh dṳ̂, sâi dṳ̂ giô̤ⁿ-seong ga-ting cīng sā̤, chā̤uⁿ ting-dêng ē chaⁿ, chā̤uⁿ hâi-bing ē sua seo̍h-ā̤uⁿ, dṳ̂ giô̤ⁿ-seong iā beoh deh siú-de̍h ē siáⁿ ceo̍ gi-gia̍h:
18 ing dî ceong-bíng Guâ ē mīng-līng, ting-ō̤ māng-go̤h bā-sa̍ⁿ beoh ciā dṳ̂ ē giô̤ⁿ-seong deh ho̤h.
19 A̍-beh-la̍h-hâng hói-táu, ga̍u i nó-cái ē ūi-chṳ̍, gah i cá̤ kî-sing ga̍u Be̍h-sī-ba. A̍-beh-la̍h-hâng cuh do̍ Be̍h-sī-ba.
20 Ca̤̍ seō î-hā̤u ū náng gah A̍-beh-la̍h-hâng gô̤ng, Bi̍h-gia iú dṳ̂-giô̤ⁿ O-seo, i-dī Bo̍-seo,
21 gah A̍-láng ē ló̤-bō̤ Gi-bâu-lī.
22 Gi-seh, Ha̍h-sô̤, Bih-da̍h, Ih-la̍h, Bî-tô-lī.
23 Cî bā̤ gá̤ náng deo sī Bi̍h-gia iú A̍-beh-la̍h-hâng ā-dī Ná-ho̤̍h hiô̤ sê̤. saⁿ ē; Bî-tó-lī saⁿ ā sîng-ná̤u-giô̤ⁿ Lī-beh-ga.
24 Ná-ho̤̍h ē ā-lî miá Liú-mâ, saⁿ Dá̤-hah, Gia-háng, Taⁿ-la̍h, Mâ-gia.
## 23
1 Sh-la̍h ho̍i-siū sia̍h seo̍h-bā d-se̍h-chih ho̍i: ca̤̍ cuh sī Sah-la̍u bāi-sa̤̍ ēho̍i-táu.
2 Sah-la̍h sî leo̍h Gi-le̍h-a̍-ba, cuh sī Gia-náng dā̤ ē Hi-beh-leóng: A̍-beh-la̍h-hâng cu̍h lí, ta̤̍ Sah-la̍h hâu cha̤-châng.
3 Î-hā̤u A̍-beh-la̍h-hâng bā-kî, lī sî-náng, gah Heh co̤̍h náng gô̤ng,
4 Guâ leo̍h dṳ̂ do̤-ng cāng-do̍ ceo̍ náng-kā; chiâⁿ dṳ̂ leo̍h dṳ̂ cî-ciâ ta̤̍h seo̍h po̍i dā̤ keoh guâ, láu ceo̍ mo̍-dā̤, ā̤ co̤̍ng guâ ma̍h-sá̤ⁿ ē sî-náng.
5 Heh co̤̍h náng i̍ng A̍-beh-la̍h-hâng gô̤ng,
6 Guâ cṳ̂, chiâⁿ tiaⁿ: dṳ̂ deó guâ do̤-ng sī da̍i ceong-gu̍i ē geong-diô̤ng: chiâⁿ leo̍h guâ mo̍-dā̤ do̤-ng gâ̤ⁿ da̍i-heô ē, co̤̍ng dṳ̂ ē sî-náng, guâ do̤-ng dih-dih beó náng gi̍ng-cî dṳ̂ dái dṳ̂ ē sî-náng leo̍h ca̤̍ mo̍-dā̤.
7 A̍-beh-la̍h-hâng kiō̤-kî, du̍i ca̤̍ dā̤-ho̤ng ē bā-sa̍ⁿ, cuh sī Heh co̤̍h náng, da̤uⁿ-uang seo̍h-sing;
8 gah i-go̤h-náng gô̤ng, Dṳ̂ seh-seô iû-sing keoh guâ co̤̍ng guâ ma̍h-sá̤ⁿ ē sî-náng, cuh meô tiaⁿ guâ ē uā, ta̤̍ guâ giú Sô̤-ha̍h ē giô̤ⁿ Î-heo̍h-leóng,
9 cio̤ng i chéng-gah Beh-bî-la̍h ē he̤̍h, gui guâ ceo̍ gi-gia̍h, sâi guâ leo̍h dṳ̂ do̤-ng ū mo̍-dā̤; i ta̤̍h ca̤̍ he̤̍h keoh guâ, guâ ngōi mua̍ⁿ ce̤h-go̤̍ gah i bâ̤.
10 Do̤ng-sí Î-heo̍h-leóng sē̤ deó Heh co̤̍h náng do̤-ng: Heh co̤̍h náng Î-heo̍h-leóng cuh leo̍h di̍h siáⁿ-mói ē Heh co̤̍h ce̤̍ng-náng mi̍ng-sá̤ⁿ, gah A̍-beh-la̍h-hâng gô̤ng,
11 Guâ cṳ̂, n̄g-sâi ah-na; chiâⁿ dṳ̂ tiaⁿ guâ gô̤ng, Guâ cio̤ng ca̤̍ chéng keoh dṳ̂; iā cio̤ng chéng hn̄g-lî ē he̤̍h keoh dṳ̂; deó guâ dó̤ng-co̤̍h ē náng mi̍ng-sá̤ⁿ, deo sa̍ng keoh dṳ̂: kô̤-î dái dṳ̂ ē sî-náng.
12 A̍-beh-la̍h-hâng cuh du̍i ca̤̍ dā̤-ho̤ng do̍ ē bā-sa̍ⁿ da̤uⁿ-uang seo̍h-sing.
13 Leo̍h hiô̤ dā̤ ē bā-sa̍ⁿ mi̍ng-sá̤ⁿ gah Î-heo̍h-leóng gô̤ng, Nō̤ ngōi dṳ̂ tiaⁿ guâ ē uā: guâ dih-dih ta̤̍h ngṳ́ng keoh dṳ̂ ceo̍ chéng-go̤̍; dṳ̂ da̤u̍h siu ca̤̍ ngṳ́ng; î-hā̤u cia̍ⁿ ta̤̍h guâ ē sî-náng dái leo̍h hṳ̂-hiô̤.
14 Î-heo̍h-leóng i̍ng A̍-beh-la̍h-hâng gô̤ng, Guâ cṳ̂, chiâⁿ tiaⁿ; ca̤̍ chéng ā̤ de̍h ngṳ́ng nn̄g-bā nâ̤u;
15 nō̤ dṳ̂ gah guâ de̤ng-gang, ū seo̍h-moi tang leōng lē? chiâⁿ dṳ̂ dái dṳ̂ ē sî-náng.
16 A̍-beh-la̍h-hâng bíng Î-heo̍h-leóng ē uā, cuh bíng Î-heo̍h-leóng dng Heh co̤̍h náng mi̍ng-sá̤ⁿ sê̤ gô̤ng ē, ta̤̍h ceo̍ seng-lî to̤ng-ē̤ng ē ngṳ́ng ching nn̄g-bā nâ̤u keoh Î-heo̍h-leóng.
17 Bíng ah-na, Î-heo̍h-leóng ēⁿ chéng deó Be̍h-bî-la̍h, deó Māng-lī du̍i-mi̍ng ē; ca̤̍ chéng, gah chéng ē he̤̍h, léng chéng do̤-ng seo̍-úi ē chiu̍-bo̤̍h, deo diāⁿ keoh A̍-beh-la̍h-hâng ceo̍ gi-ga̍h.
18 cuh sī A̍-beh-la̍h-hâng deó Î-heo̍h-leóng dó̤ng-siáⁿ ē Heh co̤̍h, náng táu-sá̤ⁿ sê̤ bâ̤ ē.
19 Î-hā̤u A̍-beh-la̍h-hâng dái i lāu-mâ Sah-la̍h leo̍h Māng-lī du̍i-mi̍ng. be̍h-bi-la̍h chéng lē ē he̤̍h: ca̤̍ Māng-lī cuh sī Hi-beh-leóng, diō̤ Gia-náng dā̤.
20 Cé̤ng ciâ kî he̤̍ chéng, léng do̤-ng ē he̤̍h, cuh sī A̍-beh-la̍h-hâng iú Heh co̤̍h náng hiô̤ sê̤ bâ̤ ē, deo diāⁿ keoh A̍-beh-la̍h-hâng ceo̍ gi-gia̍h, láu ceo̍ mo̍-dā̤.
## 24
1 A̍-beh-la̍h-hâng néng-gî lô̤-mā; beó leōng gu̍h-ôⁿ ̂ beó leōng seo̍h-moi seō, Á̤-Hó̤-Hua deo seo̍ ho̤h-ki̍ keoh i.
2 A̍-beh-la̍h-hâng gah ca̤̍ guâng i ló̤ng-co̤ng ga giua̍h ē lāu-nó-cái gô̤ng, Da dṳ̂ chiû da̤u̍h ba̍ng guâ tûi-ō̤.
3 Guâ sâi dṳ̂ gî cô-i̍ ting dā̤ Á̤-Hó̤-Hua Siō̤ng-Da̤̍ huah-sā̤, guâ do̍ Gia-náng náng do̤-ng, dṳ̂ n̄g-tang ta̤̍ guâ giô̤ⁿ teô ciâ dā̤-ho̤ng ē sîng-ná̤u-giô̤ⁿ ceo̍ lāu-mâ:
4 da̤u̍h kṳ̍ guâ bôiⁿ-dā̤ dó̤ng-co̤̍h hiô̤, ta̤̍ guâ giô̤ⁿ Î-sah teô lāu-mâ.
5 Nó-cái i̍ng gô̤ng, Seh-seô sîng-ná̤u-giô̤ⁿ n̄g-kêng do̍i guâ lí ciâ dā̤-ho̤ng; guâ dua̍ dṳ̂ giô̤ⁿ hói-táu kṳ̍ dṳ̂ sê̤ cheoh ē bôiⁿ-dā̤-ho̤ng, ā̤-sâi-dih bā̤?
6 A̍-beh-la̍h-hâng gô̤ng, Dṳ̂ da̤u̍h sa̤̍-cī, n̄g-tang dua̍ guâ giô̤ⁿ hói-táu kṳ̍ bôiⁿ-dā̤-ho̤ng.
7 Cṳ̂-câi ting-dêng ē Á̤-Hó̤-Hua Siō̤ng-Da̤̍, chio̤̍ guâ cheoh ló̤-bō̤ ē cho̍. lī bôiⁿ-co̤̍h ē dā̤. ṳ̂ng-hṳ̂ guâ; bi̍ng-chiâⁿ gah guâ huah-sā̤. gô̤ng, Guâ dih-dih ta̤̍h cî-ciâ ē dā̤ seo̍ keoh dṳ̂ giô̤ⁿ-seong; dih-dih beoh cha̤-sâi I seo̍-ciâ chio̤̍ dṳ̂, sâi dṳ̂ ā̤ ta̤̍ guâ giô̤ⁿ teô lāu mâ leo̍h bôiⁿ-dā̤-ho̤ng.
8 Seh-seó sîng-ná̤u-giô̤ⁿ n̄g-a̍i do̍i dṳ̂, ca̤̍ huah-sā̤ ē uā cuh gah dṳ̂ beó gang-geo̍: nō̤ n̄g-tang dua̍ guâ giô̤ⁿ hói-táu ga̍u bôiⁿ-dā̤-ho̤ng.
9 Nó-cái cuh cheong chiû ba̍ng i cṳ̂-cíng A̍-beh-la̍h-hâng tûi-ō̤, ing-ūi ca̤̍ seō gah i huah-sā̤.
10 Ching-chā̤uⁿ-na, nó-cái leo̍h i cṳ̂-cíng ē lo̤̍h-dó̤ do̤-ng gâ̤ⁿ se̍h táu, buh leo̍h cṳ̂-cíng ē cho̍-lî dua̍ ca̤̍u-hāng beô-bo̍i ē mo̍ih-nō̤: cuh kî-sing kṳ̍ nn̄g dá̤u eó do̤-ng ē A̍-láng, cuh ga̍u Ná-ho̤̍h sê̤ do̍ ē gā̤ⁿ-siáⁿ.
11 Di̍h-táu beoh a̍ng, cuh sī siáⁿ-lî sîng-ná̤u-giô̤ⁿ chōi chiú cûi sí-cā̤, nó-cái cuh sâi lo̤̍h-dó̤ gūi leo̍h siáⁿ-guā câⁿ-bing.
12 gô̤ng, Guâ cṳ̂-cíng A̍-beh-la̍h-hâng Siō̤ng-Da̤̍ Á̤-Hó̤-Hua ā, giú Dṳ̂ si-ṳng keoh guâ cṳ̂-cíng A̍-beh-la̍h-hâng, sâi guâ ging-nua̍ ā̤ gṳ̄-da̤u̍h neô ē gi-hōi.
13 Guâ kiō̤ ciâ câⁿ. ning, siáⁿ-lî náng ē sîng-ná̤u-giô̤ⁿ hōi chiú cûi:
14 guâ beoh giú io̍h gá̤ sîng-ná̤u-giô̤ⁿ gô̤ng, Chiâⁿ si dṳ̂ ē cúi-béng, keoh guâ chōi; nō̤ i̍ng gô̤ng, Chiâⁿ chōi, guâ iā chiú-cûi keoh dṳ̂ lo̤̍h-dó̤ sia̍h; ca̤̍ sîng-ná̤u-giô̤ⁿ ā̤ sua̍ⁿ sī Cṳ̂ sê̤ diāⁿ. bo̍i keoh Cṳ̂ nó-cái Î-sah ceo̍ lāu-nâ; iú ciâ guâ ā̤ cai Cṳ̂ ū si-ṳng eo̍h guâ cṳ̂-cíng.
15 Uā gô̤ng ho̤h bōi lâ̤u, dû-dû Lī-beh-ga chōi; ga̤ⁿ-táu lē guāⁿ ā cûi-béng, Lī-beh. ga sī Bî-tô-lī sê̤ saⁿ ē. Bî-tô-lī si A̍-beh-la̍h-hâng nâi-dā̤ Ná-ho̤̍h ē giô̤ⁿ, cuh sī Ná-ho̤̍h lāu-mâ Bi̍h-nia sê̤ cheoh ē.
16 Ca̤̍ sîng-ná̤u-diô̤ⁿ é̤ng-bāu saⁿ cīng heô, ho̤h sī sîng-ná̤u-giô̤ⁿ, bōi chiáⁿ náng: i kṳ̍ aⁿ-gíng chiú cûi, dâ̤u béng lē nuâ-muâ cuh sā̤uⁿ lí.
17 Nó-cái aâu go̍i cī i, gô̤ng, Chiâⁿ dṳ̂ béng ē ē cûi, ta̤̍h seo̍h deh-giô̤ⁿ keoh guâ sia̍h.
18 Sîng-ná̤u-giô̤ⁿ i̍ng gô̤ng, Guâ cṳ̂, chiâⁿ sia̍h: cuh guâⁿ-gîng cio̤ng cûi-béng ba̍ng ôi, ta̤̍h chiû lē, a̤̍u keoh i sia̍h.
19 Sia̍h lâ̤u, sîng-ná̤u-giô̤ⁿ buh gô̤ng, Guâ ca̍i chiú cûi keoh dṳ̂ o̤̍h-dó̤ sia̍h bô̤.
20 Sîng-ná̤u-giô̤ⁿ suh guâⁿ-gîng cio̤ng béng ē cûi, nia̍ⁿ leo̍h seó lē; buh câu kṳ̍ câⁿ lē dó̤ chiú.
21 Ca̤̍ náng duā gí-kā i. leo beó ceo̍-siaⁿ, a̍i-dí cai Á̤-Hó̤-Gua gô̤-céng ū seo̍ to̤ng-da̍h ē dō ā beó.
22 Lo̤̍h-dó̤ gi̍-céng sia̍h â̤u, ca̤̍ náng cuh ta̤̍h ging-pi̍-ke̤ng seo̍h gá̤, dāng nn̄g-cíng-bua̍ⁿ, ging-chiû-leo̍h nn̄g ko̤, dāng ngō-nâ̤u. sa̍ng i;
23 gô̤ng, Chiâⁿ dṳ̂ gah guâ. Sṳ̂ng, Dṳ̂ sī hn̍g-náng ē sîng-ná̤u-giô̤ⁿ? Dṳ̂ ló̤-bō̤ cho̍-lî ū ūi-chṳ̍ keoh guâ ā̤ do̍-má lē bā̤?
24 I i̍ng gô̤ng, Guâ sī Bî-tô-lī ē sîng-ná̤u-giô̤ⁿ, Ná-ho̤̍h gah Bi̍h-gia ē sîng-ná̤u-seong.
25 Buh gah i gô̤ng, Guâ cho̍-lî châu-lā̤u che̤ng-ce̤h, iā ū ūi-chṳ̍ keoh dṳ̂ do̍-má.
26 Ca̤̍ náng cuh da̤uⁿ-uang seo̍h-sing ba̍i Á̤-Hó̤-Hua;
27 i gô̤ng, Sā̤uⁿ-ō̤ guâ cṳ̂ A̍-beh-la̍h-hâng ē Siō̤ng-Da̤̍ Á̤-Hó̤-Hua, ing sí-sió̤ng si I ē cíng-ceó gah cing-si̍h keoh guâ cṳ̂; guâ deó dō-táu, Á̤-Hó̤-Hua chio̤̍ guâ ga̍u guâ cṳ̂ ā-dī ē cho̍-lî.
28 Sîng-ná̤u-giô̤ⁿ cuh câu kṳ̍ cho̍, ta̤̍h ca̤̍ seō gah i ná̤u-lâ̤ cho̍ lē ē náng gô̤ng.
29 Lī-beh-ga ū ā-hiaⁿ, miá La̍h-bang, câu cheoh kṳ̍, ga̍u câⁿ-bing gi̍ng ca̤̍ náng.
30 Ing-ūi kua̍ⁿ-gi̍ng pi̍-ke̤ng, iā kua̍ⁿ-gi̍ng chiû-ke̤ng dua̍ i-mo̍i chiû lē, léng tiaⁿ-mói i-mo̍i Lī-beh-ga seo̍h gô̤ng, Ca̤̍ náng ah-na gah guâ gô̤ng: cuh kṳ̍ ciū i; ca̤̍ náng ho̤h kiō̤ deó câⁿ-bing, lo̤̍h-dó̤ iā deó hiô̤.
31 La̍h-bang gah i gô̤ng, Mó̤ng Á̤-Hó̤-Hua seo̍ ho̤h-ki̍ ē náng, chiâⁿ dî; seh-na kiō̤ leo̍h guā-li̍ lē? Guâ î-ging ṳ̄-bī báng-gaⁿ lâ̤u, iā ū ta̤̍ lo̤̍h-dó̤ ṳ̄-bī sê̤-cāi.
32 Ca̤̍ náng cuh dih cho̍; La̍h-bang ta̤̍ i sia̍ lo̤̍h-dó̤ sê̤ iāⁿ ē mo̍ih-nō̤, ta̤̍h châu-lā̤u keoh i sia̍h, buh ta̤̍h cûi keoh ca̤̍ náng, léng i seo̍h bang ē náng sâ̤ ko̤.
33 lā leo̍h i mi̍ng-sá̤ⁿ bá̤ sia̍h ē mo̍ih-nō̤: nō̤ ca̤̍ náng gô̤ng, Guâ lái-i̍ bōi gô̤ng-míng, guâ n̄g-gô̤ⁿ ceo̍-sa̤ⁿ sia̍h. La̍h-bang gô̤ng, Chiâⁿ dṳ̂ gô̤ng lí.
34 Ca̤̍ náng cuh gô̤ng, Guâ sī A̍-beh-la̍h-hâng ē nó-cái.
35 Á̤-Hó̤-Hua duā seo̍-ho̤h keoh guâ cṳ̂, sâi i duā hing-ō̤ng; iā seo̍ i ging, ngṳ́̍ng, nó-cái, ā-táu, gú, á̤uⁿ, lo̤̍h-dó̤, lṳ́.
36 Guâ cṳ̂-bâ̤u Sah-la̍h, néng lô̤ sí-cā̤, saⁿ ā giô̤ⁿ keoh guâ cṳ̂; guâ cṳ̂ cio̤ng sê̤ ū ē mo̍ih-nō̤, deo gau-hu̍ keoh giô̤ⁿ.
37 Cṳ̂-cíng sâi guâ huah-sā̤ gô̤ng, Dṳ̂ n̄g-tang ta̤̍ guâ giô̤ⁿ teô guâ sê̤ do̍ Gia-náng dā̤ ē sîng-ná̤u-giô̤ⁿ ceo̍ lāu-mâ.
38 Da̤u̍h kṳ̍ guâ ló̤-bō̤ cho̍-lî bôiⁿ-co̤̍h hiô̤, ta̤̍ guâ giô̤ⁿ teô lāu-mâ.
39 Guâ i̍ng cṳ̂-cíng gô̤ng, Nō̤ giaⁿ sîng-ná̤u-giô̤ⁿ n̄g-kêng do̍i guâ lí ciâ.
40 Guâ cṳ̂-cíng cuh gô̤ng, Guâ sê̤ hō̤ng-seō ē Á̤-Hó̤-Hua, beoh sâi I ē seo̍-ciâ bô̤-hō dṳ̂, seo̍ dṳ̂ to̤ng-da̍h ē dō; dṳ̂ da̤u̍h leo̍h guâ ló̤-bō̤ cho̍-lî, bôiⁿ-co̤̍h do̤-nng, te̤̍ guâ gô̤ⁿ teô lāu-mâ̤.
41 Seh-seô dṳ̂ ga̍u guâ bôiⁿ-co̤̍h hiô̤, cuh guâ sâi dṳ̂ huah-sā̤ ē uā gah dṳ̂ beó gang-geo̍; i seh-seô n̄g ta̤̍h sîng-ná̤u-giô̤ⁿ keoh dṳ̂, ca̤̍ huah-sā̤ ē uā, iā gah dṳ̂ beó gang-geo̍.
42 Ging-nua̍ guâ ga̍u câⁿ-bing, gí-dô̤ gô̤ng, Guâ cṳ̂ A̍-beh-la̍h-hâng ē Siō̤ng-Da̤̍ Á̤-hó̤-Hua ā, Dṳ̂ seh-seô seo̍ guâ sê̤ giáⁿ ē dō ā̤ to̤ng-da̍h;
43 guâ da kiō̤ leo̍h cûi-câⁿ bing leō, gṳ̄-da̤u̍h seo̍h gá̤ sîng-ná̤u-giô̤ⁿ chōi chiú cûi, guâ chiâⁿ ta̤̍h béng lē ē cûi seo̍h-deh-giô̤ⁿ keoh guâ chōi.
44 I seh-seô i̍ng gô̤ng, Dṳ̂ meô sia̍h; guâ iā chiú keoh dṳ̂ lo̤̍h-dó̤ sia̍h; ca̤̍ sîng-ná̤u-giô̤ⁿ cuh sī Á̤-Hó̤-Hua sê̤ diāⁿ ē, po̍i keoh guâ cṳ̂ ē giô̤ⁿ ceo̍ lāu-mâ ē.
45 Guâ sing lē be̍h-be̍h gí-dô̤ ho̤h bōi lâ̤u, Lī-beh-ga dû-dû chōi, ga̤ⁿ-táu lē ngiá ā cûi-báng, kṳ̍ câⁿ lē chiú cûi: guâ cuh gah i gô̤ng, Chiâⁿ dṳ̂ ta̤̍h cûi keoh guâ chōi.
46 I cûi-béng cuh guâⁿ-gîng iú ga̤ⁿ-táu lē ba̍ng lôi, gah guâ gô̤ng, Dṳ̂ meô chōi; guâ iā chiú keoh dṳ̂ lo̤̍h-dó̤ chōi. Guâ cuh chōi, i iā chiú keoh lo̤̍h-dó̤ chōi.
47 Guâ cuh ka̍ng i gô̤ng, Dṳ̂ sī hn̍g-náng ē sîng-ná̤u-giô̤ⁿ? I i̍ng gô̤ng, Guâ sī Bî-tô-lī ē sîng-ná̤u-giô̤ⁿ, Ná-ho̤̍h gah Bi̍h-gia ē sîng-ná̤u-seong. Guâ cuh cio̤ng pi̍-ke̤ng dua̍ i pi̍ lē; chiû-leo̍h dua̍ i chiû lē.
48 Guâ seo̍h-sing da̤uⁿ-uáng ba̍i Á̤-Hó̤-Hua, buh sā̤uⁿ-guâ cṳ̂ A̍-beh-la̍h-hâng ē Siō̤ng-Da̤̍ Á̤-Hó̤-Hua; ing i chio̤̍ guâ giáⁿ cia̍ⁿ-di̍h ē dō, sâi guâ teô guâ cṳ̂ nâi-dā̤ ē sîng-ná̤u-seong keoh guâ cṳ̂ ē giô̤ⁿ ceo̍ lāu-mâ.
49 Da dṳ̂ nō̤ kêng ta̤̍h cíng-ceó cing-si̍h ka̍ng-dāi guâ cṳ̂, cuh gah guâ gô̤ng: nō̤ n̄g-kêng, iā da̤u̍h gah guâ gô̤ng, sâi guâ kô̤-î ha̤̍uⁿ leo̍h cô̤, he̍h sī ha̤̍uⁿ leo̍h iū.
50 La̍h-bang gah Bî-tô-lī cá̤ i̍ng gô̤ng, Ca̤̍ seō sī iú Á̤-Hó̤-Hua: guâ-go̤h-náng iā beó-dang-dng bēng sī-hi.
51 Lī-beh-ga deó dṳ̂ mi̍ng-sá̤ⁿ, dṳ̂ dua̍ i kṳ̍, keoh dṳ̂ cṳ̂ ē giô̤ⁿ ceo̍ lāu-mâ, bíng Á̤-Hó̤-Hua sê̤ mīng ē.
52 A̍-beh-la̍h-hâng ē nó-cái seo̍h tiaⁿ-mói ca̤̍ uā, cuh pah leo̍h dā̤-lō̤ ba̍i Á̤-Hó̤-Hua.
53 Ca̤̍ nó-cái ta̤̍h cheoh ging, ngṳ́ng ē siû-sih gah i-ná̤u sa̍ng keoh Lī-beh-ga; buh ta̤̍h bô̤-beo̍h sa̍ng keoh i-hiaⁿ léng i ná̤u-lâ̤.
54 Nó-cái gah gṳng-súi ē náng cia̍ⁿ chōi sia̍h do̍-má: ga̍u dā̤-dī cô̤ bā-kî, cuh gô̤ng, Da da̤u̍h keoh guâ dôiⁿ kṳ̍ guâ cṳ̂ hiô̤.
55 Lī-beh-ga ē ā-hiaⁿ léng i ná̤u-lâ̤ i̍ng gô̤ng, É̤ng sîng-ná̤u-giô̤ⁿ gah guâ cá̤ do̍ gûi di̍h, he̍h-chiâⁿ se̍h-di̍h hiô̤ gû; i-hā̤u cia̍ⁿ kî-síng kṳ̍.
56 Nó-cái i̍ng gô̤ng, Dṳ̂ n̄g-tang láng-cê̤ guâ. Á̤-Hó̤-Hua gi̍-céng seo̍ guâ to̤ng-da̍h ē dō; chiâⁿ dṳ̂ cuh da̤u̍h keoh guâ dôiⁿ kṳ̍ guâ cṳ̂ hiô̤.
57 Go̤h-náng i̍ng gô̤ng, Dêng guâ ga̤̍u sîng-ná̤u-giô̤ⁿ lí ka̍ng i kua̍ⁿ.
58 Cuh ga̤̍u Lī-beh-ga lí, ka̍ng i gô̤ng, Dṳ̂ kêng gah ca̤̍ náng cá̤ kṳ̍ n̄g-kêng? I i̍ng gô̤ng, Guâ sêng kṳ̍.
59 Bíng ah-na, cuh sâi i-mo̍i Lī-beh-ga, léng i neng-beô, gah A̍-beh-la̍h-hâng ē nó-cái, î-gi̍h gṳng-súi ē náng cá̤ kṳ̍.
60 Iā co̤h-ho̤h Lī-beh-ga gô̤ng, Guâ-mo̍i ā, ngōi dṳ̂ ceo̍ māng-míng ē ná̤u-lâ̤, dṳ̂ ē hā̤u-a̤̍ iā deh siú-de̍h ē siáⁿ.
61 Lī-beh-ga gah i a-táu kiô̤ lo̤̍h-dó̤ kî-sing do̍i ca̤̍ nó-cái: nó-aái cuh dua̍ Lī-beh-ga giáⁿ kṳ̍.
62 Hṳ̂ seo̍h-sí Î-sah do̍ deó náng-câⁿ lē hói-táu.
63 Bua̍ⁿ-ō̤-da̍u sí-cā̤, chōi kṳ̍ chéng lē, diāⁿ-diāⁿ hiô̤ sua̍ⁿ; gio̤̍h-kî ma̍h-ciu, kua̍ⁿ-gi̍ng o̤̍h-dó̤ lí.
64 Lī-beh-ga gio̤̍h-kî na̍h-ciu kua̍ⁿ-gi̍ng Î-sah, cuh iú o̤̍h-dó̤ lē lôi.
65 Ka̍ng nó-cái gô̤ng, Deó chéng do̤-ng giáⁿ lí cī guâ ē sī hn̍g-náng lē? Nó-cái i̍ng gô̤ng, Cuh sī guâ cṳ̂ ā: Lī-beh-ga cái cuh cio̤ng i sê̤ ceo̍
66 ē seō deo gah Î-sah gô̤ng.
67 Î-sah dua̍ Lī-beh-ga di̍h i ná̤u-lâ̤ Sah-la̍h ē da̤̍uⁿ-báng, teô i ceo̍ lāu-mâ; cīng sā̤u i. Î-sah cé̤ng i ná̤u-lâ̤ sî āu, ga̍u ciâ cia̍ⁿ deh ang-hu̍i.
## 25
1 A̍-beh-la̍h-hâng buh teô seo̍h gá̤ n̤a-sâ na̤̍h ci-da-lh2
2 Gi-do-la̍h iú A̍-beh-la̍h-hâng saⁿ Sing-láng, Io̤h-sang, Bî-dāng, Bî-déng, Ih-bah, Sṳ-a̍.
3 Io̤h-sang saⁿ Sī-ba, Dâ̤-dāng. Dâ̤-dāng hā̤u-a̤̍ sī A̍-sṳ-lī co̤̍h, Lī-do-sī go̤̍h, hī-o-bî co̤̍h.
4 Bî-déng saⁿ -hah, Î-heo̍h, Ha̍h-do̤̍h, A̍-bi̍-dāi, Î-leh-dāi. Ca̤̍ deo sī Gi-do-la̍h ē giô̤ⁿ-seong. keó oi a sao
5 A̍-beh-la̍h-hâng cio̤ng sê̤ ū ē mo̍ih-nō̤ seo̍ keoh Î-sah.
6 Dng-A̍-beh-la̍h-hâng cāi sa̤̍ sí-cā̤, î deo ū ta̤̍h mo̍ih-nō̤ seo̍ i ciô̤-sā̤ a-ē giô̤ⁿ; sâi i lī duā-giô̤ⁿ Î-sah kṳ̍ dang-bing ē dā̤.
7 A̍-beh-la̍h hâng ho̍i-siū sia̍h seo̍h-bā chih-se̍h-ngō ho̍i.
8 I néng-gî lô̤-māi, dē̤ⁿ-ku̍i sî kṳ̍, cuh gui ga̍i cô-ga.
9 I giô̤ⁿ Î-sah gah Î-si̍h-mâ-lī dái leo̍h Be̍h-bî-la̍h ē he̤̍h, deó Māng-du̍i-mi̍ng, deó Heh náng Sô̤-ha̍ giô̤ⁿ Î-heo̍h-leóng ē chéng do̤-ng;
10 ca̤̍ chéng cuh sī sâ̤ⁿ-dih A̍-beh la̍h-hâng gah Heh co̤̍h náng sê̤ bá̤ ē: A̍-beh-la̍h-hâng cuh gah i lāu-mâ Sah-la̍h ha̍h-co̤̍ng hṳ̂-hô̤.1
11 A̍-beh-la̍h-hâng sî āu, Siō̤ng--seo̍-ho̤h i giô̤ⁿ I-sah; Î-sah sê̤ do̍ ē ūi-chṳ̍ hū-gṳ̄ng La̍h-hâi-lái câⁿ lē.
12 Sah-la̍h a-táu. Ai-gi̍h sîng-ná̤u-giô̤ⁿ Hā-gah, do̍i A̍-beh-la̍h-hâng sê̤ saⁿ ē giô̤ⁿ Î-si̍h-mâ-lī; i ē hā̤u-a̤̍, bíng ci-pa̍i miá-sa̍ⁿ gi̍ deō ō̤-dâ̤:
13 Î-si̍h-mâ-lī saⁿ duā-giô̤ⁿ Ní-bái io̤h, buh saⁿ Gi-da̍h, Ah-de̍h-beh, Bi̍h-sang,
14 Bi̍h-mâ, Tô-mâ, Mâ-sah,
15 Ha̍h-da̍h, Dá̤-mâ, Ih-do̤h, Ná-hi-si̍h, Gí-dâ̤-mâ
16 iî-á do ē Io̍h-na-i dô-sē siê ē miá, bíng i ē ha̤uⁿ-che̤ng gah hô̤ⁿ-buáⁿ ceo̍ se̍h-dī co̤̍h ē bo̤̍h-beh
17 Î-si̍h-mâ-lī ho̍i-siū sia̍h seo̍h-bî so̤ⁿ-se̍h-chih ho̍i; dē̤ⁿ-ku̍i sî kṳ̍, cuh gui ga̍i cô-ga.
18 Î-si̍h-mâ-i-giô̤ⁿ-seong sê̤ do̍ ē dā̤ sī deó i ā dī dang-bing, iú Ha̍h-hi-la̍h ga̍u ai-gi̍h táu-sa̤̍ⁿ ē Sṳ-cî, di̍h-di̍h ga̍u A̍-seo̍h. A-Sa̤̍-Gi̍.ō; so.
19 A̍-beh-la̍h-hâng giô̤ⁿ Î-sah ē ga-seō gi̍ deó ō̤-dâ̤: A̍-beh-la̍h-hâng saⁿ Î-sah;
20 Î-sah si̍-se̍h ho̍i, teô Lī-beh-ga ceo̍ lāu-mâ; i sī Ba-tâng-a̍-láng dā̤, A̍-láng náng Bî-tô-lī ē sîng-ná̤u-giô̤ⁿ, A̍-láng náng La̍h-bang ē ā-mo̍i.
21 Î-sah ing i lāu-mâ bā̤ saⁿ, go̍-cheô ta̤̍ i giú Á̤-Hó̤-Hua, Á̤-Hó̤-Hua ceông i sê̤ giú; i lāu-mâ Lī-beh-ga cuh dua̍-sing.
22 Ū sang-tai deó bah-dî duā dôiⁿ-dāng, chā̤uⁿ seo-caⁿ sseo̍h-ā̤uⁿ; Lī-beh-ga gô̤ng, Seh-seô sī ah-na, guâ deoh-nâi ching-chā̤uⁿ na lē? I cuh kṳ̍ ka̍ng Á̤-Hó̤-Hua.
23 Á̤-Hó̤-Hua gô̤ng, tu Nn̄g go̤h diō̤ dṳ̂ tai lē do̤-ng; Nn̄g co̤̍h iú dṳ̂ bah-dô-lî cheoh; Cî seo̍h co̤̍h beoh ā̤ gió̤ng hṳ̂ seo̍h co̤̍h; Duā-giô̤ⁿ beoh ho̤̍h-seō dā̤-dī-giô̤ⁿ.
24 Ga̍u leo̍h-dā̤ di̍h-gí, gô̤-céng sī sang-tai.
25 No̤̍ saⁿ cheoh-si̍ ē sing áng, to̤ng-sing ū nńg, chā̤uⁿ pói-so̤ⁿ seo̍h ā̤uⁿ; cuh heō miá ga̤̍u Î-sô̤.
26 Deo̍-bôi cheoh-si̍ ē, chiû kiáng Î-sô̤ ko̤-āu-daⁿ; cuh heō miá ga̤̍u Ngâ-go̤h. Saⁿ ca̤̍ nn̄g gá̤ giô̤ⁿ sí-cā̤, I-sah î-ging la̍h-se̍h ho̍iaṳ̂u
27 Nn̄g gá̤-giô̤ⁿ ciāng-ciāng diô̤ng-duā: I-sô̤ duā sai-hū dô̤-lo̤̍h, le̍ng leo̍h ko̤̍ng-iâ; Ngâ-go̤h po̤h-si̍h, gṳ-do̤ do̍ leo̍h da̤̍uⁿ-báng.
28 Î-sah sā̤u I̍-sô̤, ing Î-sô̤ gṳ-do̤ ta̤̍h iâ-bī ge̤ng-gih i sia̍h: nō̤ Lī-beh-ga sā̤u Ngâ-go̤h.
29 Ū seo̍h di̍h Ngâ-go̤h hiô̤ cṳ̂ dāu-eng: dû-dû Î-sô̤ iú ko̤̍ng-iâ hói-táu, cīng sing-kô.
30 I-sô̤ cuh gah Ngâ-go̤h gô̤ng, Guâ cīng sing-kô ā; chiâⁿ dṳ̂ ta̤̍h ca̤̍ áng ē tng keoh guâ sia̍h. Go̍-cheô I-sô̤ buh miá ga̤̍u leō Î- do̤ng. (î-do̤ng huang-i̍h cuh sī áng ē i̍-seo̍)
31 Ngâ-go̤h cuh gô̤ng, Dṳ̂ cio̤ng ca̤̍ diô̤ng-ceô míng-heōng ging-nua̍ bā̤ keoh guâ.
32 Î-sô̤ gô̤ng, Guâ beoh sî leō: ca̤̍ diô̤ng-ceô ē míng-heōng keoh guâ ū seo̍h-moi iā lē?
33 Ngâ-go̤h gô̤ng, Ging-nua̍ da̤u̍h gah guâ huah-sā̤. Î-sô̤ cuh gah i huah-sā̤: cio̤ng diô̤ng-ceô ē míng-heōng bā̤ keoh Ngâ-go̤h.
34 Ngâ-go̤h cuh ta̤̍h biâⁿ léng áng-dāu-tng keoh Î-sô̤; Î-sô̤ sia̍h chōi, cuh bā-kî giáⁿ kṳ̍. Bíng ah-na, Î-sô̤ kua̍ⁿ-king diô̤ng-ceô ē míng-heōng.
## 26
1 DDā̤ Da̤uⁿ. Dng A̍-beh-la̍h-hâng sí-cā̤, táu seo̍h hói ū gi-hng lâ̤u; Î-sah sí-cā̤, ca̤̍ dā̤ buh ū gi-hng: ing-cheô I-sah kṳ̍ Gi-la̍h, ga̍u Hi-lī-seō náng ē ó̤ng A̍-bi̍-bî-leh hṳ̂-hiô̤.
2 Á̤-Hó̤-Hua cheoh-hēng gah I-sah gô̤ng, Dṳ̂ n̄g-tang kṳ̍ Ai-gi̍h; nō̤ do̍ leo̍h Guâ sê̤ cî-sī ē ūi-chṳ̍.
3 Dṳ̂ cāng-sí do̍ deó ciâ dā, Guâ ā̤ bô̤-hō dṳ̂, seo̍-ho̤h dṳ̂: ing Guâ ta̤̍h le̍h-go̤h seo̍ dṳ̂, léng dṳ̂ hā̤u-a̤̍ Guâ sá̤ⁿ-di̍h gah dṳ̂ ló̤-bō̤ A̍-beh-la̍h-hâng sê̤ huah-sā̤ ē. Guâ dih-dih bíng ca̤̍ uā.
4 Guâ beoh ga-ting dṳ̂ ē hā̤u-a̤̍, chā̤uⁿ ting-dêng ē chaⁿ hṳ̂-muâng sā̤; cio̤ng ca̤̍ le̍h-go̤h seo̍ keoh dṳ̂ hā̤u-a̤̍; teng-hā māng-go̤h ē bā-sa̍ⁿ deo dih-dih iú dṳ̂ hā̤u-a̤̍ deh ho̤h-ki̍.
5 Ca̤̍ sī ing A̍-beh-la̍h-hâng tiaⁿ bíng Guâ ē uā, dng Guâ ē cih-seō, sî Guâ ē ga̍i-mīng, leo̍h-lā̤, hah-dō.
6 Î-sah do̍ deó Gi-la̍h.
7 Hiô̤ dā̤-ho̤ng ē bā-sa̍ⁿ ka̍ng ga̍u i ē lāu-mâ; i cuh gô̤ng, Sī guâ ē ā-mo̍i; ing Lī-beh-ga é̤ng-bāu bā̤u-li̍, go̍-cheô n̄g-gô̤ⁿ ching i ceo̍ lāu-mâ, giaⁿ cî-ciâ ē bā-sa̍ⁿ ing Lī-beh-ga ē é̤ng-go̍ ā̤ tái i.
8 Deó hṳ̂-hiô̤ do̍ chu̍i gû, Hi-lī-seō ē ó̤ng A̍-bi̍-bî-leh, iú te̤ng lē kua̍ⁿ chōi, gi̍ng Î-sah gah i lāu-mâ Lī-beh-ga cá̤ hiô̤ lāng-sâ̤u.
9 A̍-bi̍-bî-leh cuh dā̤u Î-sah lí, gô̤ng, I gô̤-céng sī dṳ̂ lāu-mâ: seh-na gô̤ng sī dṳ̂-mo̍i lē? Î sah i̍ng gô̤ng, Guâ a̍ng-āu sua̍ⁿ, giaⁿ lē ing i ē é̤ng-go̍, guâ ā̤ keoh náng tái kṳ̍.
10 A̍-bi̍-bî-leh gô̤ng, Dṳ̂ seh-na ah-na dāi guâ-go̤h-náng lē? Bā-sa̍ⁿ do̤-ng he̍h-chiâⁿ ū náng A-gah dṳ̂ lāu-mâ cá̤ keo̍ng, dṳ̂ cuh ā sai gâ-go̤h-náng hāng-sūi.
11 Á-ua-lâ-le̍h bah leong-uah bā-sa̍ⁿ gô̤ng. Háng bō̤-hāng ca̤̍ náng. gah i lāu-mâ ē náng, dih-dih bāng i sî-cōi.
12 Î-sah deó hiô̤ dā̤ geng-ce̤̍ng. cî seo̍h-níng siu-síng deh seo̍h-bā bōi: Á̤-Hó̤-Hua seo̍ ho̤h-ki̍ keoh i.
13 Cé̤ng ciâ cuh duā chio̤ng-sīng. di̍h-di̍h ga-ting. siáⁿ ceo̍ duā cái-cô.
14 Ū gú. á̤uⁿ, siáⁿ-geóng, sâi-do̍-gī i.
15 Dng i ló̤-bō̤ A̍-beh-la̍h-hâng sí-cā̤, ló̤-bō̤ ē nó-cái sê̤ lê̤ ē câⁿ, î-hā̤u Hi-lī-seō náng teh ca̤̍ câⁿ, ē̤ng tó teo̍ng kṳ̍.
16 A̍-bi̍-bî-leh gah Î-sah gô̤ng, Dṳ̂ ā̤ gió̤ng-duā guâ. ió̤ⁿ geo̍ guâ-go̤h-náng, chiâⁿ dṳ̂ lī guâ-go̤h-náng.
17 I-sah cuh lī cî-ciâ, deó Gi-la̍h suaⁿ-eo̍ dō̤ da̤̍uⁿ-báng do̍.
18 I ló̤-bō̤ A̍-beh-la̍h-hâng sí-cā̤ sê̤ lê̤ ē câⁿ, ga̍u A̍-beh-la̍h-hâng sî āu, keoh Hi-lī-seō náng teo̍ng kṳ̍, Î-sah buh lê̤ dôiⁿ, ta̤̍h ló̤-bō̤ sê̤ ga̤̍u ē miá heō ca̤̍ câⁿ.
19 Î-sah ē nó-cái geo̍h câⁿ leo̍h suaⁿ-eo̍ do̤-ng, deh seo̍h gá̤ ua̍h-cûi ē uâⁿ.
20 Gi- la̍h chi̍ táu-saⁿ ē náng gah Î-sah chi̍ táu-saⁿ ē náng seo-caⁿ, gô̤ng, Ca̤̍ cûi sī se̤̍h guâ ē: ing i seo-caⁿ, go̍-cheô miá ca̤̍ câⁿ ga̤̍u A̍i-seh.
21 i Buh ca̍i kui seo̍h gá̤ câⁿ, iā cá̤ seo-caⁿ; go̍-cheô miá ga̤̍u Sih-teh-ná. GSih-teh-ná huang-i̍h cuh sī dâ̤-de̍h ē i̍-gī)
22 Lī hṳ̂-hiô̤, buh kui seo̍h gá̤ câⁿ, cuh beó ca̍i seo-caⁿ: go̍-cheô miá ga̤̍u Lī-hó̤-beh; i̍-seo̍ gô̤ng, Á̤-Hó̤-Hua sâi guâ kuang-sṳ, go̍-cheô ā̤ chio̤ng-sīng leo̍h ca̤̍ dā̤.
23 Î-sah cuh lī hṳ̂-hiô̤ kṳ̍ Be̍h-sī-ba.
24 Cî seo̍h má Á̤-Hó̤-Hua hêng-hēng gah i gô̤ng, Guâ sī Dṳ̂ ló̤-bō̤ A̍-beh-la̍h-hâng ē Siō̤ng-Da̍; n̄g-sâi giaⁿ, ing Guâ nó-cái A̍-beh-la̍h-hâng ē é̤ng-go̍. Guâ dih-dih bô̤-hō dṳ̂, seo̍-ho̤h leo̍h dṳ̂, sâi dṳ̂ hā̤u-a̤̍ cīng sā̤.
25 Î-sah leo̍h hṳ̂-hiô̤ de̤h ca̤̍-dái peh-cheh giú Á̤-Hó̤-Hua ē miá, cuh dō̤ da̤̍uⁿ-báng;, i nó-cái leo̍h hṳ̂-hiô̤ lê̤ câⁿ
26 A̍-bi̍-bî-leh gah i béng-iû A̍-hō-sah, léng cio̤ng geong Hi-go̤h. cé̤ng Gi-la̍h lí gi̍ng Î-sah.
27 Î-sah gah i gô̤ng, Dṳ̂-go̤h-náng sá̤ⁿ-di̍h hiáng guâ, ga̤̍u guâ lī dṳ̂ kṳ̍; da seh-na buh lí gi̍ng guâ lē?
28 i̍ng gô̤ng, Guâ-go̤h-náng gi̍ng Á̤-Hó̤-Hua bô̤-hō dṳ̂: cuh gông, bû gah guâ de̤ng-gang ing-do̤ng lh-sā̤, cá̤ seo geh-méng.
29 Sá̤ⁿ-dih guâ-go̤h-náng n̄g-beh ga-hāi leo̍h dṳ̂, sī cīng hā̤u ka̍ng-dāi dṳ̂, sa̍ng dṳ̂ bíng-ang kṳ̍ cho̍; bíng ah-na, dṳ̂ iā n̄g-tang ta̍h gái dāi guâ;, da dṳ̂ ū mó̤ng Á̤-Hó̤-Hua seo̍-ho̤̍h.
30 Î-sah cuh bāng-ciû chiâⁿ i; go̤h-náng cá̤ chōi sia̍h.
31 Dā̤-dī cô̤ bā-kî, cá̤-cá̤ huah-sā̤; Î-sah sa̍ng i bíng-bíng-ang kṳ̍ cho̍.
32 Cî seo̍h di̍h, Î-sah ē nó-cái lí, ta̤̍h kui ca̍ⁿ ē seō lí beo̍ gô̤ng, Guâ deh da̤u̍h cûi ā:
33 cuh ching ca̤̍ cûi ga̤̍u Sī-ba; go̍-cheô hṳ̂-hiô̤ ē siáⁿ, ga̍u da ho̤h miá Be̍h-sī-ba.
34 I-sô̤ si̍-se̍h ho̍i sí-cā̤, teô Heh co̤̍h náng Bî-lī ē sîng-ná̤u-giô̤ⁿ Iú-deh, gah Heh co̤̍h náng Î-leóng ē sîng-ná̤u-giô̤ⁿ Ba-si̍h-buah ceo̍ lāu-mâ.
35 Ca̤̍ nn̄g gá̤ sîng-ná̤u-giô̤ⁿ sâi Î-sah gah Lī-beh-ga ē sing do̤-do̤ deh chá̤u-kô.
## 27
1 ā̤ i Ca̤uⁿ. I-sah néng lô̤, ma̍h-ciu bū, bā̤ kua̍ⁿ-gi̍ng, cuh ga̤̍u duā-giô̤ⁿ I-sô̤, gô̤ng, Guâ giô̤ⁿ ā: i i̍ng gô̤ng, Guâ deó cî-ciâ.
2 I-sah gô̤ng, Guâ lāu leō, n̄g-cai sî leo̍h deoh-seo̍h di̍h.
3 Dṳ̂ tang gio̤̍h go̤-li, ge̤ng gah ci̍ng, kṳ̍ ko̤̍ng-iâ, ta̤̍ guâ dô̤-lo̤̍h; deh iâ-siu̍ ceo̍ ā ga-háu, ha̍h guâ sê̤ ho̤̍ ē, páng lí keoh guâ sia̍h; te̍ng guâ ho̤h bōi sî, ta̤̍ dṳ̂ ceh-ho̤h.
4 Î-hā̤u Î-sô̤ kṳ̍ ko̤̍ng-iâ, a̍i-dí dô̤-lo̤̍h iâ-siu̍ ga̍u cho̍. Î-sah heong-nu̍ giô̤ⁿ Î-sô̤ sí-cā̤, Lī-beh-ga ū tiaⁿ-mói.
5 Lī-beh-ga cuh gah i giô̤ⁿ Ngâ-go̤h gô̤ng, Guâ tiaⁿ-mói dṳ̂ ló̤-bō̤ heong-nu̍ dṳ̂-hiaⁿ Î-sê̤ gô̤ng,
6 Ta̤̍ guâ dô̤-lo̤̍h iâ-siu̍ ceo̍ ā ga-háu keoh guâ sia̍h, te̍ng guâ bōi sî sí-cā̤, guâ leo̍h Á̤-Hó̤-Hua mi̍ng-sá̤ⁿ ta̤̍ dṳ̂ ce̤h-ho̤h.
7 
8 Da guâ giô̤ⁿ da̤u̍h tiaⁿ guâ ē uā, bíng guâ sê̤ mīng-līng ē.
9 Dṳ̂ kṳ̍ á̤uⁿ-geóng lē, keng búi ē suaⁿ-á̤uⁿ-giô̤ⁿ nn̄g táu lí; guâ ta̤̍ dṳ̂ ló̤-bō̤ ceo̍ ā ga-háu, ha̍h i sê̤ ho̤̍ ē:
10 dṳ̂ cuh páng keoh dṳ̂ ló̤-bō̤ sia̍h, te̍ng i bōi sî. ta̤̍ dṳ̂ ce̤h-ho̤h.
11 Ngâ-go̤h i̍ng ná̤u-lâ̤ Lī-beh-ga gô̤ng, Guâ-hiaⁿ seo̍h-sing ū mn̍g; guâ sī gng-geo̍h-geo̍h.
12 Seh-seô ló̤-bō̤ mo guâ, sua̍ⁿ guâ. sī pe̍ng ló̤-bō̤, giaⁿ lē giú ce̤h-ho̤h, huâng gi̍ng siū mo̤̍.
13 I ná̤u-lâ̤ gô̤ng, Guâ giô̤ⁿ ā, dṳ̂ nō̤ siū mo̤̍, guâ ga̍i dng kṳ̍; dṳ̂ nō̤ tiaⁿ guâ ē uā, kṳ̍ keng ca̤̍ á̤uⁿ-giô̤ⁿ.
14 Cuh kṳ̍ keng lí keoh i ná̤u-lâ̤; ná̤u-lâ̤ cuh ceo̍ ā ga-háu, ha̍h i ló̤-bō̤ sê̤ ho̤̍ ē.
15 Lī-beh-ga ta̤̍h kn̍g deó cho̍-lî duā-giô̤ⁿ Î-sô̤ bā̤u-li̍ ē i-ná̤u keoh dā̤-dī giô̤ⁿ Ngâ-go̤h sē̤ng:
16 buh ta̤̍h suaⁿ-á̤uⁿ-giô̤ⁿ ē pói, ma i chiû gah dāu-l̤ gng-geo̍h ē ūi-chṳ̍:
17 ta̤̍h sê̤ bāng ē ga-háu gah biâⁿ, gau i giô̤ⁿ Ngâ-go̤h chiû lē.
18 Ngâ-go̤h ci̍ng keoh ló̤-bō̤, gô̤ng, Guâ ló̤-bō̤ aī. I i̍ng gô̤ng, Guâ-deó cî-ciâ; dṳ̂ sī hn̍g-náng?
19 Nĝ-go̤h i̍ng ló̤-bō̤ gô̤ng, Guâ sī duā-giô̤ⁿ Î-sô̤; guâ bíng ló̤-bō̤ sê̤ mīng-līng ē uā ceo̍ lâ̤u: chiâⁿ bā-kî sē̤, sia̍h guâ sê̤ dô̤-lo̤̍h ē, ta̤̍ guâ ce̤h-ho̤h.
20 Î-sah ka̍ng giô̤ⁿ gô̤ng, Guâ giô̤ⁿ, dṳ̂ seh-na ā̤ deh da̤u̍h ciô̤ gîng lē? l̍ng gô̤ng, Dṳ̂ ē Siō̤ng-Da̤̍ Á̤-Hó̤-Hua sâi guâ deh da̤u̍h ca̤̍.
21 Î-sah gah Ngâ-go̤h gô̤ng, Guâ giô̤ⁿ, gṳ̄ng sá̤ⁿ lí; guâ a̍i-dí mo dṳ̂, kua̍ⁿ gô̤-céng sī guâ giô̤ⁿ Î-sô̤ ā n̄g-sī.
22 Ngâ-go̤h cuh giáⁿ sá̤ⁿ, ga̍u i ló̤-bō̤ Î-sah mi̍ng-sá̤ⁿ; Î-sah mo i gô̤ng, Siaⁿ-ing sī Ngâ-go̤h ē siaⁿ-ing, chiû buh sī Î-sô̤ ē chiû.
23 Ing i chiû lēu mńg, chā̤uⁿ i-hiaⁿ Î-sô̤, go̍-cheô ló̤-bō̤ bā̤ bēng dih cheoh: cuh ta̤̍ i ce̤h-ho̤h.
24 Buh ka̍ng gô̤ng, Dṳ̂ cing-cia̍ⁿ sī guâ giô̤ⁿ Î-sô̤ lō̤? h̍g gô̤ng, Cia̍ⁿ-sī.
25 Buh gô̤ng, Guâ giô̤ⁿ sê̤ deh ē bá̤ leo̍h guâ mi̍ng-sá̤ⁿ, dêng guâ sia̍h lō̤, ta̤̍ dṳ̂ ce̤h-ho̤h. Ngâ-go̤h cuh páng keoh ló̤-bō̤, ló̤-bō̤ sia̍h ca̤̍: buh ta̤̍h ciû keoh ó̤-bō̤ chōi.
26 Î-sah gô̤ng, Guâ giô̤ⁿ, táu-sá̤ⁿ lí, gah guâ cing-chu̍i.
27 Cuh giáⁿ sá̤ⁿ gah ló̤-bō̤ cing-chu̍i; ló̤-bō̤ bī i-ná̤u pang-pang, cuh ta̤̍ i ce̤h-ho̤h, gô̤ng, Á̤-Hó̤-īng pang, guâ giô̤ⁿ ē pang iā ah-na.
28 Nō̤ ngōi Siō̤ng-Da̤̍ seo̍ dṳ̂ ing lē ē gang-lo̍, dā̤ lē ē búi-tó, ĝ-go̤h gah ciû che̤ng-ce̤h;
29 e̍h-go̤h ho̤̍h-seō dṳ̂, māng-co̤̍h sa̍i-gūi dṳ̂; hiaⁿ-dī ceong dṳ̂ ceo̍ sṳ̂, gah dṳ̂ ceo̍ seo̍h ná̤u-lâ̤ ē náng gūi-ba̍i dṳ̂: mo̤̍ dṳ̂ ē náng, gi̍ng mo̤̍ dôiⁿ, ce̤h-ho̤h dṳ̂ ē náng, gi̍ng ce̤h-ho̤h dóiⁿ.
30 I-sah ce̤h-ho̤h Ngâ-go̤h lâ̤u, cua kaû lh té̤he-i̤̍-lá eh-ā̤ k̂a-a Ngâ-go̤h lī ló̤-bō̤ cheoh kṳ̍, dû-dû hiaⁿ Î-sô̤, dô̤-lo̤̍h hói-táu;
31 iā eeo̍ ga-háu, páng di̍h ló̤-bō̤ hiô̤. gô̤ng, Chiâⁿ ló̤-bō̤ bā-kî, sia̍h guâ sê̤ dô̤-lo̤̍h ē, î-di̍ ta̤̍ guâ ce̤h-ho̤h.
32 I ló̤-bō̤ Î-sah ka̍ng gô̤ng, Dṳ̂ sī m̍g-náng lē? D̍g gô̤ng, Guâ sī dṳ̂ duā-giô̤ⁿ, Î-sô̤.
33 I-sah duā giaⁿ, lā̤u-dā̤u-ce̍ng gô̤ng, Dṳ̂ bōi lí ē kî-áu, hn̍g-náng ta̤̍h ca̤̍ iâ-siu̍ ceo̍. ga-háu páng di̍h keoh guâ? Guâ sia̍h lâ̤u, î-ging ta̤̍ i ce̤h-ho̤h; i dih-dih ā̤ deh ca̤̍ ho̤h-ki̍.
34 I-sô̤ tiaⁿ-mói ca̤̍ uā, cuh duā-siaⁿ hâu, cīng sô-tia̍ⁿ, gah ló̤-bō̤ gô̤ng, Chiâⁿ ló̤-lō̤ iā ta̤̍ guâ ce̤h-ho̤h.
35 Ló̤-bō̤ gô̤ng, Dṳ̂-dī lí, ē̤ng kûi-ga̤̍ dua̍h dṳ̂ ē ce̤h-ho̤h.
36 Î-sô̤ gô̤ng, I miá. ga̤̍u Ngâ-go̤h, (gâ-go̤h sī ki-ca̍ ē. ggī n̄g iā ha̍h-gí lō̤? I pe̍ng guâ nn̄g hói lâ̤u: sá̤ⁿ-di̍h dua̍h guâ duā-giô̤ⁿ ē míng-heōng; da dua̍h guâ ē. ce̤h-ho̤h. Buh gô̤ng, Meh beó beh-uuⁿ ē ho̤h ta̤̍ guâ ce̤h-ho̤h lō̤? sah
37 i̍ng Î-sô̤ gô̤ng, Guâ î-ging i̍h Ngâ-go̤h ceo̍ dṳ̂ ē cṳ̂, sâi ciô̤- gó hiaⁿ-dī deo ho̤̍h-seō i; buh ta̤̍ gô-go̤h gah ciû seo̍ i; guâ giô̤ⁿ guâ da ā̤ ta̤̍h seo̍h-moi mo̍ih-nō̤ se keoh dṳ̂ lē?
38 Î-sô̤ gah i ló̤-gô̤ng, Ló̤-bō̤ ē co̤h-ho̤h meh nō̤ seo̍h hāng lō̤? Giú ló̤-bō̤ ca̍i ta̤̍ gu ce̤h-ho̤h ā. Cuh kui-siaⁿ duā hâu.
39 I-gi̍h eu̍ sâ̤-u Só dâ cô ē ūi-cho̤̍, ca̤̍ tó búi heô, buh ā̤ de ting-dêng ē gang-lo̍;
40 dṳ̂ beo. ciā deo-gia̍ng bá̤u-seng, ho̤̍h-seo leo̍h dṳ̂-dī; ga̍u dṳ̂ gió̤ng-sīng A-cā̤, cia̍ⁿ ā̤ tuah-lī i ē lá̤-do̤ⁿ leo̍. dṳ̂ dāu-lṳ lē.
41 Î-sô̤ ing ló̤-bō̤ ta̤̍ Ngâ-go̤ ce̤h-ho̤h, cuh hṳ̄ng Ngâ-go̤h, sing-lî siô̤ng gô̤ng, Ga̍u ló̤-bō̤ so̤ng-seō sí-cā̤, gui dih-dih tái guâ-dī Ngâ-go̤h.
42 Ū náng ta̤̍h duā-giô̤ⁿ A-sô̤ ē uā, to̤ng-di Lī-beh-ga; Lī-beh-ga cuh sâi náng ga̤̍u i dā̤-dī giô̤ⁿ Ngâ-go̤h lí, gah i gô̤ng, Dṳ̂-hiaⁿ Î-sô̤ a̍i-dí tái dṳ̂, ta̤̍h cheoh i ē siū-ki̍.
43 Guâ giô̤ⁿ, da̤u̍h tiaⁿ guâ ē uā, câu kṳ̍ Ha̍h-láng, dáu guâ-hiaⁿ La̍h-bang hiô̤.
44 Hē̤ hiô̤ gûi di̍h, dêng dṳ̂-hiaⁿ ē siū-ki̍ suā kṳ̍.
45 Ga̍u dṳ̂-hiaⁿ ē siū-ki̍ suā kṳ̍, beoh-gi̍-leh dṳ̂ sê̤ ceo̍ ē seō, guâ cuh sâi náng ga̤̍u dṳ̂ iú hṳ̂-hiô̤ hoi-táu: meh tang leo̍h seo̍h di̍h se̤̍ng-sih kṳ̍ dṳ̂ nn̄g gá̤ náng lō̤?
46 Lī-beh-ga gah Î-sah gô̤ng, Guâ ing ca̤̍ Heh co̤̍h ē sîng-ná̤u-giô̤ⁿ, ia̍ng-ki̍ guâ ē ua̍h-miā; seh-seô Ngâ-go̤h iā teô hiô̤ dā̤-ho̤ng Heh co̤̍h ah-na ē sîng-ná̤u-giô̤ⁿ ceo̍ lāu-mâ, guâ láu ca̤̍ miā ū seo̍h-moi iā̤ lē? aa a̤ a
## 28
1 I-sah ga̤̍u Ngâ-go̤h lí, ta̤̍ i ce̤h-ho̤h, heong-nu̍ i gô̤ng, N̄g-tang teô Gia-náng ē sîng-ná̤u-giô̤ⁿ a ceo̍ lāu-mâ.
2 Da̤u̍h-bā-kî kṳ̍ dia-ū, tâng-a̍-láng, ga̍u dṳ̂ ná̤u-lâ̤ ē ló̤-bō̤ Bî-tô-lī cho̍-lî; iú dṳ̂-gū La̍h-ē bang ē sîng-ná̤u-giô̤ⁿ do̤-ng teô isîng-ná̤u-giô̤ⁿ ceo̍ lāu-mâ.
3 Ngōi cé̤ng-néng ē Siō̤ng-Da̤̍ seo̍-ho̤h leo̍h dṳ̂, sâi dṳ̂ hing-ō̤ng, chio̤ng-o̍sīng, ceo̍ go̤h-co̤̍h ē cô-co̤ng.
4 Buh ta̤̍h sê̤ ṳ̂ng A̍-beh-la̍h-hâng ē hho̤h seo̍ keoh dṳ̂ léng dṳ̂ hā̤u-a̤̍; ta̤̍h sê̤ seo̍ A̍-beh-la̍h-hâng ē dā̤, cuh sī dṳ̂ sê̤ gio̤̍-do̍ ē dā̤, seo̍ keoh dṳ̂ ceo̍ gi-gia̍h.
5 Bíng ah-na, Î-sah sâi Ngâ-go̤h kṳ̍ Ba-tâng-a̍-láng, dáu A̍-láng náng Bî-tô-lī ē giô̤ⁿ La̍h-bang, cuh sī Ngâ-go̤h Î-sô̤ ná̤u-lâ̤ Lī-beh-ga ē ā-hiaⁿ.
6 Î-sô̤ gi̍ng Î-sah ta̤̍ Ngâ-go̤h ce̤h-ho̤h, sâi i kṳ̍ Ba-tâng-a̍-láng, a̍i-dí i deó hṳ̂-hiô̤ teô lāu-mâ; ta̤̍ i ce̤h-ho̤h sí-cā̤, heong-nu̍ i gô̤ng, N̄g-tang teô Gia-náng ē sîng-ná̤u-giô̤ⁿ ceo̍ lāu-mâ;
7 buh gi̍ng Ngá-go̤h bíng ló̤-bō̤ ná̤u-lâ̤ ē mīng-līng, kṳ̍ Ba-tâng-a̍-láng:
8 bíng ah-na, Î-sô̤ cai Gia-náng ē sing-ná̤u-giô̤ⁿ bā̤ huaⁿ-hî leo̍h ló̤-bō̤ Î-sah;
9 go̍-cheô kṳ̍ Î-si̍h-mâ-lī cho̍-lî, diō̤ nn̄g gá̤ lāu-mâ î-gōi, buh teô Mâ-ha̍h-la̍h ceo̍ lāu-mâ; Mâ-ha̍h-la̍h sī A̍-beh-la̍h-hâng ē sîng-ná̤u-seong, Î-si̍h-mâ-lī ē sîng-ná̤u-giô̤ⁿ, Ní-ba̍i-io̤h ē ā-mo̍i.
10 Ngâ-go̤h lī Be̍h-sī-ba, hio̤̍ng ūi-chṳ̍, ing di̍h-táu
11 î-ging leo̍h suaⁿ, cuh leo̍h hiuô̤ do̍-má, ta̤̍h ciâ ūi-chṳ̍ seo̍h po̍i sa̤u̍h ceo̍ cîng-táu, deô hiô̤ keo̍ng.
12 Ceo̍-ma̍ng gi̍ng ū láu-tui kio̤̍ dā̤-lō̤, tui-bôi ta̍ng ga̍u ting lē: Siō̤ng-Da̤̍ ē seo̍-ciâ sā̤uⁿ-leo̍h leo̍h dêng-ni̍.
13 Á̤-Hó̤- Guâ sī Á̤-Hó̤-Hua, cuh sī dṳ̂ cô-co̤ng A̍-beh-la̍h-ha̍ng ē Siō̤ng-Da̤̍, gah Î-sah ē Siō̤ng-Da̤̍: dṳ̂ sê̤ keo̍ng ē dā̤, Guâ deo seo̍ keoh dṳ̂, léng dṳ̂ hā̤u-a̤̍.
14 Dṳ̂ ē hā̤u-a̤̍ dih-dih cīng sā̤; chā̤uⁿ dā̤ lē ē tó-suaⁿ, sua̍ⁿ kui do̤ng, sa̤, náng, bo̤h: ting-ō̤ māng-míng dih-dih ing dṳ̂ léng dṳ̂ ē hā̤u-a̤̍ deh ho̤h-kí.
15 Beó leōng dṳ̂ kṳ̍ deoh-leo, Guâ dih-dih bô̤-hō dṳ̂, ca̤̍u-go̍ dṳ̂; iā sâi dṳ̂ hói-táu ciâ dā̤-ho̤ng; deo beó n̄g-dí dṳ̂; Guâ sê̤ ṳ̂ng-hṳ̂ dṳ̂ ē seō, dih-dih bíng ca̤̍ uā.
16 Ngâ-go̤h ching-châⁿ, cuh gô̤ng, Á̤-Hó̤-Hua gô̤-céng deó cî-ciâ; nō̤ guâ n̄g-cai.
17 Cuh cīng giaⁿ, gô̤ng, Ciâ dā̤-ho̤ng seh-na ā̤ na tang giaⁿ! Ca̤̍ n̄g-sī Siō̤ng-Da̤̍ ē dēng, ting ē mói lō̤?
18 Ngâ-go̤h cô̤-cô̤ bā-kî, ta̤̍h ceo̍ cîng-táu ē sa̤u̍h kio̤̍ kî ceo̍ tiū, deo̍ iú leo̍h sa̤u̍h dêng-ni̍.
19 Cuh heō hiô̤ ūi-chṳ̍ ga̤̍u leō Be̍h-de̍h-lī, (Be̍h-de̍h-lī huang-i̍h cuh sī Siō̤ng-Da̤̍ cho̍ ē i̍-gī ca̤̍ siáⁿ ngói-miá sī ga̤̍u Lō-seo.
20 Ngâ-go̤h cuh huah-ngē̤ng gô̤ng, Seh-seô Siō̤ng-Da̤̍ diō̤ guâ sê̤ giáⁿ ē dō, bô̤-hō ca̤̍u-go̍ guâ, seo̍ guâ i-si̍h,
21 sâi guâ bíng-ang hói-táu ga̍u guâ ló̤-bō̤ ē cho̍, cuh guâ ceong-gi̍ng Á̤-Hó̤-Hua Cṳ̂ ceo̍ guâ ē Siō̤ng-Da̤̍.
22 Guâ sê̤ kio̤̍ ceo̍ tiū ē sa̤u̍h, dih-dih ceo̍ Siō̤ng-Da̤̍ ē dēng; háng Siō̤ng-Da̤̍ sê̤ seo̍ guâ ē, guâ dih-dih se̍h heōng tiu seo̍h heōng, he̤̍ng keoh Siō̤ng-Da̤̍.
## 29
1 Noâ-go̤h cuh kî-sing, ga̍u dang-bing náng ē dā̤.
2 Gi̍ng chéng do̤-ng ū câⁿ, ū á̤uⁿ so̤ⁿ dīng, pah deó câⁿ-bing; ing náng gṳ-do̤ iú ciâ câⁿ lē keoh á̤uⁿ-dīng sia̍h cûi, câⁿ-chu̍i ē̤ng duā sa̤u̍h ka̤̍ⁿ.
3 Ga̍u go̤h dīng ē á̤uⁿ deo cṳ̄-ci̍h hṳ̂-hiô̤, n̍g á̤uⁿ ē náng cuh chia sa̤u̍h lī ca̤̍ câⁿ-chu̍i, chiú cûi keoh á̤uⁿ sia̍h; súi-āu cíng-céng ta̤̍h ca̤̍ sa̤u̍h ka̤̍ⁿ câⁿ-chu̍i.
4 Ngâ-go̤h ka̍ng gô̤ng, Hiaⁿ-dī, dṳ̂ iú deoh-leo li? I i̍ng gô̤ng, Guâ iú Ha̍h-láng lí.
5 Buh ka̍ng gô̤ng, Dṳ̂ beh Ná-ho̤̍h ē seong La̍h-bang n̄g-beh? I̍g gô̤ng, Guâ beh i.
6 Ngâ-go̤h gô̤ng, I deo heô lō̤? I̍g gô̤ng, Deo heô: da i ē sîng-ná̤u-giô̤ⁿ La̍h-geh, iā dua̍ á̤uⁿ-dīng lí.
7 Ngâ-go̤h gô̤ng, Di̍h-táu ho̤h chu̍i gá̤ⁿ, n̄g-sī cṳ̄-ci̍h á̤uⁿ-dīng ē sí-cā̤; n̄g-cṳ́ ta̤̍h cûi keoh á̤uⁿ sia̍h, ca̍i kṳ̍ n̍g i.
8 I̍g gô̤ng, Bā̤, da̤u̍h dêng go̤h dīng á̤uⁿ cṳ̄-ci̍h, cia̍ⁿ ā̤ chia sa̤u̍h lī ca̤̍ câⁿ-chu̍i keoh á̤uⁿ sia̍h cûi.
9 Ngâ-go̤h gah ce̤̍ng-náng gô̤ng sí-cā̤, La̍h-geh dua̍ á̤uⁿ ga̍u lâ̤u; ing ló̤-bō̤ ē á̤uⁿ sī i n̍g.
10 Ngâ-go̤h kua̍ⁿ-gi̍ng i ná̤u-lâ̤-gū La̍h-bang ē sîng-ná̤u-giô̤ⁿ La̍h-geh, gah ná̤u-lâ̤-gū La̍h-bang ē á̤uⁿ-dīng, cuh giáⁿ sá̤ⁿ chia sa̤u̍h lī câⁿ-chu̍i, keoh i ná̤u-lâ̤-gū La̍h-bang ē á̤uⁿ sia̍h cûi.
11 Ngâ-go̤h gah La̍h-geh seo-gi̍ng giaⁿ-lâ̤ lâu, cuh kuî-siaⁿ hâu.
12 Ngâ-go̤h gah La̍h-geh gô̤ng, Guâ sī dṳ̂ ló̤-bō̤ ē gōi-seng, Lī-beh-ga ē giô̤ⁿ. La̍h-geh cuh câu kṳ̍ gah i ló̤-bō̤ gô̤ng.
13 La̍h-bang seo̍h tiaⁿ-mói i gōi-seng Ngâ-go̤h ga̍u, cuh câu kṳ̍ cī i, peō i dāu-lṳ, gah i cing-chu̍i, chio̤̍ i di̍h cho̍. Ngâ-go̤h cuh cio̤ng ló̤ng-cô̤ng ē seō, gô̤ng keoh La̍h-bang tiaⁿ.
14 La̍h-bang gah i gô̤ng, Dṳ̂ si̍h-si̍h sī guâ ci̍-dui do̍ ū seo̍h go̍ih-di̍h.
15 La̍h-bang gah Ngâ-go̤h gô̤ng, Dṳ̂ sui- ̄ si̍ guâ ci̍-ching, meh tang sâ. dṳ̂ ba̍h-ba̍h ta̤̍ guâ ceo̍ seō? dṳ̂ meô gah guâ gô̤ng, a̍i-dí deh seo̍h-mo gang-cíng?
16 La̍h-bang ū nn̄g sá̤ sîng-ná̤u-giô̤ⁿ: duā ē miá Lī-a̍, sa̤̍ ē miá La̍h-geh.
17 Lī-a̍ ē ma̍h-ciu ū bāⁿ; La̍h-geh é̤ng-bāu saⁿ cīng heô.
18 Ngâ-go̤h sā̤u La̍h-geh: cuh i̍ng La̍h-bang gô̤ng, Guâ ing dṳ̂ sa̤̍ ē sîng-ná̤u-giô̤ⁿ La̍h-geh, ta̤̍ dṳ̂ ceo̍ seō chih níng.
19 La̍h-bang gô̤ng, Ta̤̍h sîng-ná̤u-giô̤ⁿ go̤̍ keoh beh-náng, n̄g-cṳ́ go̤̍ keoh dṳ̂: dṳ̂ meô gah guâ cá̤ do̍.
20 Ngâ-go̤h ing La̍h-geh ceo̍ seō chih níng: ing da̍i sā̤u La̍h-geh, kua̍ⁿ chih níng chā̤uⁿ gûi di̍h.
21 Ngâ-guh gah La̍h-bang gô̤ng, Cṳ̍ dih-gí î-ging ga̍u lâ̤u, chiâⁿ ta̤̍h guâ ē lāu-mâ dá̤ⁿ guâ, sâi guâ síng-ching.
22 La̍h-bang cû bāng-ciû, chiâⁿ hṳ̂-hiô̤ ching-iiú cṳ̄-ci̍h
23 Ga̍u ting a̍ng, chio̤̍ sîng-ná̤u-giô̤ⁿ Lī-a̍ ga̍u Ngâ-go̤h hiô̤: Ngâ-go̤h cuh gah i ceo̍ seo̍h báng.
24 La̍h-bang buh ta̤̍h a-táu Sih-pa̍ keoh i sîng-ná̤u-giô̤ⁿ Lī-a̍ ceo̍ a-táu.
25 Dā̤-dī cô̤, Ngâ-go̤h kua̍ⁿ-gi̍ng i, cai sī Lī-a̍, cuh gah L a̍h-bang gô̤ng, Seh-na ka̍ng dāi guâ ching-chā̤uⁿ na lē? Guâ n̄g-sī ing La̍h-geh ta̤̍ dṳ̂ ceo̍, s̤ lêⁿ Dṳ̂ i̍-á se̍ng gâ lē!
26 La̍h-bang gô̤ng, Duā ē bōi cheoh-go̤h, sa̤̍ ē sa̤ⁿ chiáⁿ, guâ cî-ciâ dā̤-ho̤ng bōi ū ah-na ceo̍.
27 Gai-d̍ng gah Lî-a̍ cá̤ muâ chih di̍h ē gí, guâ iā ta̤̍h La̍h-geh poi keoh dṳ̂; dṳ̂ da̤u̍h ca̍i ta̤̍ guâ ceo̍ seō chih bíng.
28 Ngâ-go̤h bíng i ē uā muâ chih di̍h ē gí: La̍h-bang bu cio̤ng La̍h-geh po̍i keoh i ceo̍ lāu-má.
29 La̍h-bang ta̤̍h a-táu Bi̍h-la̍h keoh i sîng-ná̤u-giô̤ⁿ La̍h-geh ceo̍ a-táu.
30 Ngâ-go̤h iā gah La̍h-geh ceo̍ seo̍h báng; sā̤u La̍h-geh piāⁿ Lī-a̍ ho̤h ā̤ sā̤; cíng-céng buh ta̤̍ La̍h-bang ceo̍ seō chih níng.
31 Á̤-Hó̤-Hua cai Lī-a̍ bā̤ deh dah-bo sā̤u, go̍-cheô sâi i ā̤ dua̍-sing: nō̤ La̍h-geh bā̤ saⁿ.:
32 Lī-a̍ dua̍-sing saⁿ giô̤ⁿ, ching i miá ga̤̍u Liú-bēng: Giú-bēng, huang-i̍h cuh sī mó̤ng ka̍ng-go̍ deh giô̤ⁿ ē i̍-gī,)i na gô̤ng, Á̤-Hó̤-Hua gô̤-céng gi̍-niāng guâ ē kô-cíng; da î-hā̤u, dah-bo dih-dih sā̤u guâ.
33 Buh dua̍-sing saⁿ giô̤ⁿ, gô̤ng, Á̤-Hó̤-Hua cai guâ bā̤ deh dah-bo sā̤u, seo̍ guâ ca̍i saⁿ ā giô̤ⁿ; cuh ching i miá ga̤̍u Sa̤-me̍ng. (Sa̤-me̍ng, huang-i̍h cuh sī tiaⁿ-mói cai ē Î-gī.)
34 Buh dua̍-sing saⁿ giô̤ⁿ; gô̤ng, ing guâ ū saⁿ so̤ⁿ gá̤ giô̤ⁿ lâ̤u, daih-bo da dih-dih ā̤ gah guâ hó̤-ha̍h: go̍-cheô ching i miá ga̤̍u Lī-bī, Gī-bī, huang-i̍h cuh sī hó̤-ha̍h ē i̍-gī.)
35 Buh dua̍-sing saⁿ giô̤ⁿ, gô̤ng, Guâ da dih-dih sā̤uⁿ-ō̤ Á̤-Hó̤-Hua: go̍-cheô ching i miá ga̤̍u Iú-dāi. Gú-dāi, huang-i̍h cuh sī sē̤ng-bî ē i̍-gī) Î-hā̤u Lī-a̍ dē̤ⁿ chu̍i-gû beó a̤u.3
## 30
1 Hah-geh gi̍ng ga̍i do̍i Ngâ-go̤h bā̤ saⁿ giô̤ⁿ, cuh ci̍h-do̍ i-cî; gah Ngâ-go̤h gô̤ng, Da̤u̍h seo̍ guâ saⁿ giô̤ⁿ; nō̤ n̄g ah-na, guâ ā̤ sî.
2 Ngâ-go̤h cuh siū-ki̍ La̍h-geh; gô̤ng, Sâi dṳ̂ bā̤ saⁿ sī Siō̤ng-Da̤̍; guâ meh ā̤ ta̤̍ Siō̤ng-Da̤̍ lō̤?
3 La̍h-geh gô̤ng, Guâ ē a-táu Bi̍h-la̍h gah dṳ̂ ceo̍ seo̍h cho̍; i saⁿ ē giô̤ⁿ, guâ hú-iô̤ng i leo̍h gô-táu, guâ iú i deh ā giô̤ⁿ.
4 Cuh ta̤̍h a-táu Bi̍h-la̍h keoh lāu-go̤ng ceo̍ á-lî: Ngâ-go̤h gah i ceo̍ seo̍h cho̍.
5 Bi̍h-la̍h do̍i Ngâ-go̤h dua̍-sing saⁿ giô̤ⁿ.
6 La̍h-geh gô̤ng, Siō̤ng-Da̤̍ ū ta̤̍ guâ sing-e̤ng, tiaⁿ guâ gí-dô̤, seo̍ guâ cî seo̍h gá̤ giô̤ⁿ: go̍-cheô ching i miá ga̤̍u Dāng. CDāng, cuh sī sing-e̤ng ē i̍-gī.)
7 La̍h-geh ē a-ta̍u Bi̍h-la̍h, do̍i Ngâ-go̤h buh dua̍-sing saⁿ dā̤-dī giô̤ⁿ.
8 La̍h-geh gô̤ng, Guâ gah guâ-cî duā seo-caⁿ, cuh deh-si̍ng: go̍-cheô ching i miá ga̤̍u Da̍h-dāi-lī. (Da̍h-dāi-lī, huang-i̍h cuh sī seo-caⁿ ē i̍-gī.)
9 Lī-a̍ gi̍ng beông-sing beó ca̍i a̤u, cuh cio̤ng i ē a-táu Sih-pa̍ keoh beông-hu ceo̍ a-lî.
10 Lī-a̍ ē a-táu Sih-pa̍ do̍i Ngâ-go̤h saⁿ giô̤ⁿ.
11 Lī-a̍ gô̤ng, Ho̤h-ki̍ ga̍u leō: go̍-cheô ching i miá ga̤̍u Gia-deh GGia-deh, huang-i̍h cuh sī ho̤h-k ē i̍-gī.)
12 Lī-a̍ ē a-táu Sih-pa̍, do̍i Ngâ-go̤h saⁿ dā̤-dī giô̤ⁿ.
13 Lī-a̍ gô̤ng, Guâ ū ho̤h-ki̍ ā! hiô̤-sā̤ ē sîng-ná̤u-giô̤ⁿ beoh ching guâ ū ho̤h-ki̍; go̍-cheô ching i miá ga̤̍u A̍-seh. (A̍-seh, huang-i̍h cuh sī ching ho̤h ē i̍-gī.)
14 Ga̍u guā ba̍h sí-cā̤, Liú-bēng kṳ̍ chéng lē, deh da̤u̍h dō-dāi, (Dō-dāi sī cha̍i ē miá, se̤̍h ga-cha̍i lūi.) ta̤̍h keoh i ná̤u-lâ̤ Lī-a̍. La̍h-geh gah Lī-a̍ gô̤ng, Chiâⁿ dṳ̂ ta̤̍h dṳ̂ giô̤ⁿ ē dō-dāi keoh guâ.
15 Lī-a̍ gô̤ng, Dṳ̂ dua̍h guâ dah-bo, meh sī sâ̤u-kô̤ ē seō lō̤? Da buh a̍i-dí cia̍ng guâ giô̤ⁿ ē dō-dāi lō̤? La̍h-geh gô̤ng, Ing dṳ̂ giô̤ⁿ ē dō-dāi, ging-má dah-bo ā̤ gah dṳ̂ cá̤ keo̍ng.
16 Ga̍u bua̍ⁿ-ō̤-da̍u, Ngâ-go̤h iú chéng lē hói-táu, Lī-a̍ chōi kṳ̍ cī i, gô̤ng, Dṳ̂ da̤u̍h lí guâ cî-ciâ; ing guâ ta̤̍h guâ giô̤ⁿ ē dō-dāi chiâⁿ dṳ̂. Ngâ-go̤h cî seo̍h má cuh gah i cá̤ do̍-má.
17 Siō̤ng-Da̤̍ tiaⁿ ṳ̂ng Lī-a̍; Lī-a̍ cuh dua̍-sing, saⁿ dā̤-ngō̤ gá̤ giô̤ⁿ.
18 Lā-a̍ gô̤ng, Guâ sá̤ⁿ-di̍h ta̤̍h a-táu keoh dah-bo, mó̤ng Siō̤ng-Da̤̍ seo̍ guâ gang-cíng; go̍-cheô ching i miá ga̤̍u I. sah-gia. (Î-sah-gia, huang-i̍h cuh sī deh gang-cíng ē i̍-gī.)
19 Lī-a̍ buh dua̍-sing, do̍i Ngâ-go̤h saⁿ dā̤-la̍h gá̤ giô̤ⁿ.
20 Lī-a̍ cuh gô̤ng, Siō̤ng-Da̤̍ seo̍ guâ hā̤u ē co̤ng-liáng; guâ cé̤ng dah-bo saⁿ la̍h gá̤. giô̤ⁿ; da î-hā̤u dah-bo dih-dih gah guâ cá̤ do̍: go̍-cheô ching i miá. ga̤̍u Sa̤-bo̍-leóng. (Sa̤-bo̍-leóng, huang-u̍h cuh sī cá̤ do̍ ē i̍-gī?
21 e-ngng t̍hu̍h sī ṳ̍hô i tīih.. Î-hā̤u buh saⁿ seo̍h gá̤ sîng-ná̤u-giô̤ⁿ, ching i miá ga̤̍u Dâ̤-ná.
22 Siō̤ng-Da̤̍ gi̍-niāng La̍h-geh. sâi i ā̤ saⁿ.
23 I cuh dua̍-sing saⁿ giô̤ⁿ, gô̤ng, Siō̤ng-Da̤̍ i-ging dṳ́ guâ ē sa̤u-lâ̤.
24 Cuh ching i miá ga̤̍u Io̤h-seh; (Io̤h-seh huang-ih cuh sī ga-ting ē i̍-gī.) ing-ūi sī na. gô̤ng, Siō̤ng-Da̤̍ dih-dih buh ca̍i seo̍ guâ seo̍h gá̤ giô̤ⁿ.
25 La̍h-geh saⁿ Io̤h-seh āu, Ngâ-go̤h gah La̍h-bang gô̤ng, Chiâⁿ cha̤-sâi guâ hói-táu kṳ̍ guâ bôiⁿ-dā̤ bôiⁿ-ha̤uⁿ.
26 Chiâⁿ dṳ̂ ta̤̍h guâ ta̤̍ dṳ̂ ceo̍ seō sê̤ deh ē bô giô̤ⁿ keoh guâ, cha̤-kêng guâ kṳ̍ cho̍: ing guâ seo̍h-moi ā̤uⁿ ta̤̍ dṳ̂ ceo̍ seō, dṳ̂ deo cai lâ̤u.
27 La̍h-bang gah i gô̤ng, Guâ bíng-so̍ cai Siō̤ng-Da̤̍ ing-ūi dṳ̂ cuh seo̍ ho̤h-ki̍ keoh guâ; cio̤̍h-céng mó̤ng dṳ̂ ē ṳng, cuh gah guâ cá̤ do̍.
28 Buh gô̤ng, Chiâⁿ dṳ̂ io̤h diāⁿ gang-cíng, guâ cuh ta̤̍h keoh dṳ̂.
29 Ngâ-go̤h i̍ng i gô̤ng, Guâ seo̍h-moi ā̤uⁿ ta̤̍ dṳ̂ ceo̍ seō, n̍g dṳ̂ ciô̤-sā̤ táu-saⁿ, sī dṳ̂ sê̤ cai ē.
30 Dng guâ bōi lí sí- tâ̤, dṳ̂ sê̤ ū ē mo̍ih-nō̤ cīng câ̤u; ga̍u guâ lí-dṳ̂ ciâ āu, Á̤-Hó̤-Hua seo̍-ho̤h keoh dṳ̂, ga-ting cīng sā̤; guâ leo̍h seo̍h-moi sí-cā̤ cia̍ⁿ ā̤ ta̤̍ ga̍i síng-ga li̍h-gia̍h lē?
31 La̍h-bang gô̤ng, Guâ da̤u̍h ta̤̍h seo̍h-moi keoh dṳ̂ lē? Ngâ-go̤h i̍ng gô̤ng, Dṳ̂ n̄g-sâi ta̤̍h seo̍h-moi keoh guâ: bí-do̤̍h ū seo̍h hāng ē seō ṳ̂ng-síng guâ, guâ cuh cíng-céng chi̍ ni̍g dṳ̂ ē á̤uⁿ.
32 Guâ ging-nua̍ muâ-muâ ca-diâng dṳ̂ ē á̤uⁿ-dīng; háng ū bang, ū hua-diâng, léng cang-seh ē míng-á̤uⁿ, léng ū bang, ū hua-diâng ē suaⁿ-á̤uⁿ, deo gâ̤ⁿ chōi, cuh ta̤̍h ca̤̍ ceo̍ guâ ē gang-cíng.
33 Aū-di̍h dṳ̂ ca-chah guâ deh ceo̍ gang-cíng ē á̤uⁿ-geóng: háng suaⁿ-á̤uⁿ n̄g-sī ū bang, ū hua-diâng, míng-á̤uⁿ n̄g-si cang-seh ē, dṳ̂ cuh gí guâ sī tau-ta̤̍h ē: bíng ah-na, guâ ē go̤ng-gī tng ta̍h caí ceo̍ -ei̍ng-ge̍ng.
34 La̍h-bang gô̤ng, Tang bíng dṳ̂ sê̤ gô̤ng ē uā kṳ̍ ceo̍.
35 Dng hṳ̂ seo̍h di̍h, La̍h-bang cio̤ng suaⁿ-á̤uⁿ-gô ū meóng-lî, ū diâng ē, suaⁿ-á̤uⁿ-beô ū diâng, ū bang, léng ū ga̤u ba̍h-meóng ē, gah cang-seh míng-á̤uⁿ ē giô̤ⁿ, deo gâ̤ⁿ chōi, gau leo̍h ga̍i-á̤ ē giô̤ⁿ,
36 sâi ca̤̍ á̤uⁿ-dīng lī Ngâ-go̤h ū so̤ⁿ-di̍h ē dō: Ngâ-go̤h n̍g La̍h-bang sê̤ sīng ē á̤uⁿ.
37 Ngâ-go̤h cuh ta̤̍h ió̤ng-liû, hēng-chiu̍, boiⁿ-chiu̍ ē nôi-gi, bo̤h-pói siáⁿ ā meóng, sâi ca̤̍ nôi-gi ē ba̍h-seh hēng chōi.
38 Ta̤̍h sê̤ bo̤h ē gi ba̍ng leo̍h chi̍-á̤uⁿ ē cûi-gau cûi-seó lē, gah á̤uⁿ seo du̍i-sa̤̍uⁿ: sâi i lí sia̍h sí-cā̤, gang beô seo gau-gâng.
39 Á̤uⁿ du̍i ca̤̍ gi seo gau-gâng, cuh saⁿ ū meóng-lî, ū diâng, ū leng ē áuⁿ-giô̤ⁿ.
40 Ngâ-go̤h cio̤ng ca̤̍ á̤uⁿ-giô̤ⁿ beong kui, sâi La̍h-bang á̤uⁿ-geóng gah ca̤̍ ū meóng-lî, ke̤ng-diâng, léng cang-seh ē á̤uⁿ seo-du̍i; ta̤̍h ga̍i beông-sing ē á̤uⁿ līng-gōi ba̍ng seo̍h ūi-chṳ̍, n̄g gah La̍h-bang ē á̤uⁿ seo-ga̤u.
41 Gṳ̄-da̤u̍h búi-co̤̍ng ē á̤uⁿ gau-ha̍h sí-cā̤, Ngâ-go̤h cuh cio̤ng ca̤̍ gi ba̍ng leo̍h cûi-gau hn̄g-lî, sâi á̤uⁿ du̍i da̤u̍h ca̤̍ gi cuh gau-ha̍h.
42 Á̤uⁿ soi-cio̤̍h ē sí-cā̤, cuh n̄g ba̍ng ca̤̍ gi: cé̤ng ciâ kî, soi-cio̤̍h ē, gui da̤u̍h La̍h-bang, búi-co̤̍ng ē, gui da̤u̍h Ngâ-go̤h.
43 Bíng ah-na, Ngâ-go̤h cīng cái-cô, ū hiô̤-sā̤ á̤uⁿ-dīng, nó-cái, a-táu, lo̤̍h-dó̤, lṳ́.
## 31
1 Lah-bang go̤h-gá̤ giô̤ⁿ gî-leōng gô̤ng, Guâ lō̤-bō̤ sê̤ ū ē mo̍ih--nō̤, keoh Ngâ-go̤h deo châ̤uⁿ kṳ̍; i ciā guâ ló̤-bō̤ sê̤ ū ē deh ca̤̍ duā cái-cô Ngâ-go̤h tiaⁿ-mói ca̤̍ uā.
2 Ngâ-go̤h buh gi̍ng La̍h-bang ē mi̍ng-seh, ha̤̍uⁿ i n̄g-chā̤uⁿ sá̤ⁿ-di̍h seo̍h-ā̤uⁿ.
3 Á̤-Hó̤-Hua heong-nu̍ Ngâ-go̤h gô̤ng, Dṳ̂ da̤u̍h dôiⁿ kṳ̍ dṳ̂ ā-go̤ng ē dā̤, po̤ⁿ-dôiⁿ dṳ̂ bôiⁿ-co̤̍h; Guâ dih-dih bô̤-hō
4 dṳ̂.4 Ngâ-go̤h sâi náng ga̤̍u La̍h-geh, Lī-a̍ ga̍u chéng lē, cuh sī n̍g á̤uⁿ-dīng ē ūi-chṳ̍,
5 gah i gô̤ng, Guâ gi̍ng dṳ̂ ló̤-bō̤ ē mi̍ng-seh, ha̤̍uⁿ guâ n̄g chā̤uⁿ sá̤ⁿ-di̍h; nō̤ guâ ló̤-bō̤ ē Siō̤ng-Da̤̍ sí-sió̤ng bô̤-hō guâ.
6 Guâ cīng li̍h hō̤ng-seō dṳ̂ ló̤-bō̤, dṳ̂ ā̤ cai lâ̤u.
7 Dṳ̂ ló̤-bō̤ pe̍ng guâ, se̍h hói iō̤ⁿ guâ ē gang-cíng, keh-hēng Siō̤ng-Da̤̍ n̄g é̤ng i hāi guâ.
8 I cio̤̍h-seô gô̤ng, Ū hua-diâng ē keoh guâ ceo̍ gang-cíng; ca̤̍ á̤uⁿ-dīng sê̤ saⁿ ē deo ū hua-diâng; cio̤̍h-seô gô̤ng, Ū meóng-lî ē keoh guâ ceo̍ gang-ci̍ng, cuh cé̤ á̤uⁿ-dīng sê̤ saⁿ ē deo ū meóng-lî
9 Ah-na sī Siō̤ng-Da̤̍ cio̤ng dṳ̂ ló̤-bō̤ ē táu-saⁿ seo̍ keoh guâ.
10 Dng á̤uⁿ-dīng gau-ha̍h sí-cā̤, guâ ceo̍-ma̍ng do̤-ng gi̍ng kió̤ á̤uⁿ-beô ē á̤uⁿ-gô deo sī ū meóng-lî hua-diâng gah ū bang ē.
11 Siō̤ng-Da̤̍ ē seo̍-ciâ to̤h-ma̍ng ga̤̍u gua gô̤ng, Ngâ-go̤h. Guâ i̍ng gô̤ng, Guâ deó cî-ciâ.
12 Seo̍-ciâ gô̤ng, Dṳ̂ gio̤̍h-kî ma̍h-ciu kua̍ⁿ, kió̤ á̤uⁿ-beô ē á̤uⁿ-gô deo sī ū meóng-lî, ū hua-diâng, ū bang; ing La̍h-bang seh-na dāi dṳ̂, Guâ î-ging deó kua̍ⁿ-gi̍ng lâ̤u.
13 Guâ sī Be̍h-de̍h-lī ē Siō̤ng-Da̤̍, dṳ̂ sá̤ⁿ-di̍h deó hiô̤, ta̤̍h iú dó ca̤̍ tiū, leo̍h Guâ táu-sá̤ⁿ huah-ngē̤ng: da da̤u̍h bā-kî lī ciâ dā̤-ho̤ng, dôiⁿ kṳ̍ dṳ̂ boiⁿ-co̤̍h ē dā̤.
14 La̍h-geh, Lī-a̍ i̍ng gô̤ng, Guâ leo̍h ló̤-bō̤ cho̍-lî, meh ho̤h ū i̍ng-heōng ē gia̍h-táu lō̤? meh ho̤h ū tang sio̤̍ ē gau-bôi lō̤?
15 Guâ ló̤-bō̤ gi̍-céng bā̤ guâ; ching-teong guâ ē gang-cíng, n̄g-sī kua̍ⁿ guâ gah guā-li̍ ē náng seo̍h-ā̤uⁿ lō̤? Siō̤ng-Da̤̍ ta̤̍h guâ ló̤-bō̤ ē ho̤̍-cái seo̍ guâ, deo se̤̍h leo̍h guâ, léng guâ giô̤ⁿ.
16 Da Siō̤ng-Da̤̍ sê̤ heong-hu̍ dṳ̂ ē, dṳ̂ deo da̤u̍h bíng.
17 Ngâ-go̤h cuh kî-sing sâi bô giô̤ⁿ kió̤ lo̤̍h-bó̤, dua̍ i sê̤ ū ē táu-saⁿ, léng sê̤ deh ē ho̤̍-cái:
18 cuh sī deó Ba-tâng-a̍-láng sê̤ deh ē, kṳ̍ Gia-náng dā̤, gi̍ng ló̤-bō̤ Î-sah.
19 Dû-dû La̍h-bang kṳ̍ go̤ á̤uⁿ-mńg; La̍h-geh tau-a̤̍u i ló̤-bō̤ cho̍-lîē gâ̤u-siō̤ng.
20 Ngâ-go̤h cia-muá A̍-láng náng La̍h-bang, beó geh gô̤ng cuh tau uang câu.
21 Cuh kî-giáⁿ, dua̍ sê̤ ū ē mo̍ih-nō̤, câu kṳ̍; geo̍ eó, ha̤̍uⁿ Gi-le̍h suaⁿ giáⁿ. A-Sa̤̍-Gi̍.i̍: î.
22 Ga̍u dā̤-so̤ⁿ di̍h, ū náng cio̤ng Ngâ-go̤h câu-deó gah La̍h-bang gô̤ng,
23 La̍h-bang cuh dua̍ ciô̤-gá̤ hiaⁿ-dī dui i; dui ū chih di̍h, ga̍u Gi-le̍h suaⁿ cia̍ⁿ de̤̍h da̤u̍h.
24 Cî̤ seo̍h má, A̍-láng náng La̍h-bang mma̍ng Siō̤ng-Da̤̍ ga̍u i hiô̤, gô̤ng, Da̤u̍h sa̤̍-cī, n̄g-tang gah Ngâ-go̤h gô̤ng heô lō̤, gô̤ng gái lō̤.
25 La̍h-bang dui da̤u̍h Ngâ-go̤h, Ngâ-go̤h î-ging dō̤ da̤̍uⁿ-báng leo̍h Gi-le̍h suaⁿ; La̍h-bang gah ciô̤-gá̤ hiaⁿ-dī iā dō̤ da̤̍uⁿ-báng leo̍h Gh-le̍h suaⁿ.
26 La̍h-bang gah Ngâ-go̤h gô̤ng, Dṳ̂ seh-na ceo̍? dṳ̂ cia-muá guâ, dua̍ guâ ē sîng-ná̤u-giô̤ⁿ kṳ̍, gah gio̤̍h deo châ̤uⁿ-giah ē náng seo̍h-ā̤uⁿ?
27 Dṳ̂ seh-na tau-câu, muá guâ tau kṳ̍? n̄g gah guâ ha̍ⁿ, sâi guâ cha̤̍uⁿ-geo, duáⁿ-gíng, pā-gô, huaⁿ-huaⁿ-hî-sa̍ng dṳ̂ kṳ̍?
28 Seh-na n̄g é̤ng guâ gah náng-dṳ̂-giô̤ⁿ cing-chu̍i? Dṳ̂ sê̤ ceo̍ ē si̍h-cāi sī ngō̤ng.
29 Guâ ē li̍h ū-dang-dng hāi dṳ̂: nō̤ dṳ̂ ló̤-bō̤ ē Siō̤ng-Da̤̍, seo̍h-má heong-nu̍ guâ gô̤ng, Da̤u̍h sa̤̍-cī, n̄g-tang gah Ngâ-go̤h gô̤ng heô lō̤, gô̤ng gái lō̤.
30 Dṳ̂ seo-bō ló̤-bō̤ ē cho̍, a̍i-dí dôiⁿ kṳ̍, ā̤-sâi-lē; seh-na buh tau-a̤̍u guâ ē síng-siō̤ng lē?
31 Ngâ-go̤h i̍ng La̍h-bang gô̤ng, Guâ bah-dî sua̍ⁿ, giaⁿ dṳ̂ beoh châ̤uⁿ dṳ̂ sîng-ná̤u-giô̤ⁿ lī guâ, go̍-cheô giaⁿ.
32 Nō̤ dṳ̂ síng-siō̤ng beó leōng hn̍g-náng a̤̍u, cho̍i da̤u̍h, cuh n̄g é̤ng i ua̍h: dng ciô̤-gá̤ hiaⁿ-dī mi̍ng-sá̤ⁿ, dṳ̂ gi̍ng diō̤ ciâ ū seo̍h-moi se̤̍h dṳ̂ ē. cuh meô ta̤̍h kṳ̍. Ing Ngâ-go̤h si̍h-cāi n̄g-cai La̍h-geh ū tau-ta̤̍h La̍h-bang ē síng-siō̤ng.
33 La̍h-bang cuh di̍h Ngâ-go̤h da̤̍uⁿ-báng, léng Lī-a̍ da̤̍uⁿ-báng, gah nn̄g gá̤ a-táu ē da̤̍uⁿ-báng; cho̍i deo beó. Cuh ih. Hī-a̍ da̤̍uⁿ-báng lē chōi, di̍h La̍h-geh da̤̍uⁿ-báng lē.
34 La̍h-geh ta̤̍ gâ̤u-siō̤ng ba̍ng deó lo̤̍h-dó̤ uaⁿ Ii ṳ̄-dâ̤, ga̍i sē̤ deó mi̍ng-dêng. La̍h-bang to̤ng da̤̍uⁿ-báng sa̤u geo̍, cho̍i deo beó.
35 La̍h-geh gah i ló̤-bō̤ gô̤ng, Guâ go̍ih-ging dû-dû ga̍ⁿ, bā̤ kiō̤ bā-kî cī; chiâⁿ guâ cṳ̂ n̄g-tang siū-ki̍. La̍h-bang muâ cho̍i ca̤̍ gâ̤u-siō̤ng, ga̍u-bôi cho̍i beó.
36 Ngâ-go̤h cuh siū-ki̍, ceh-bī La̍h-bang gô̤ng, Guâ ū seo̍h-moi cōi-ke̤ng, siū dṳ̂ ah-na gîng-gih dui guâ?
37 Da dṳ̂ muâ-muâ sa̤u guâ ē mo̍ih-nō̤; háng se̤̍h leo̍h dṳ̂ cho̍-lî ē mo̍ih-nō̤, dṳ̂ ū cho̍i da̤u̍h, cuh tang ba̍ng ca̤̍ mo̍ih-nō̤ leo̍h guâ-go̤h-náng ciô̤-gá̤ hiaⁿ-dī mi̍ng-sá̤ⁿ, sâi i bēng-be̍h dṳ̂ guâ ē sī-hi.
38 Guâ gah dṳ̂ seo̤̍h-dui dī-se̍h míng; dṳ̂ beô ē míng-á̤uⁿ suaⁿ-á̤uⁿ bōi ū la̍u-sing; dṳ̂ gang ē míng-á̤uⁿ, guâ iā n̄g-beh sia̍h.
39 Keoh iâ-siu̍ gō̤ sî ē, iā beó gui keoh dṳ̂, guâ ga̍i bói-tiah: di̍h-dng má-láu keoh che̍h tau kṳ̍ ē, dṳ̂ hio̤̍ng guâ chiû lē teô.
40 Guâ di̍h-dng siū ce̍h, má-láu siū lêng; ma̍h-ciu keo̍ng iā bā̤ leo̍h-míng.
41 Guâ leo̍h dṳ̂ cho̍ lē ho̤̍h-seō dṳ̂ dī-se̍h níng; ing-ūi dṳ̂ nn̄g gá̤ sîng-ná̤u-giô̤ⁿ se̍h-si̍ níng; buh ing-ūi dṳ̂ táu-saⁿ la̍h níng: nō̤ dṳ̂ se̍h hói iō̤ⁿ guâ ē gang-cíng.
42 Seh-seô guâ ló̤-bō̤ ē Siō̤ng-Da̤̍, cuh sī A̍-beh-la̍h-hâng ē Siō̤ng-Da̤̍, Î-sah sê̤ giaⁿ-u̍i ē Siō̤ng-Da̤̍, n̄g bô̤-hō guâ, dṳ̂ cuh ā̤ sâi guâ kang-chiû dôiⁿ kṳ̍; nō̤ Siō̤ng-Da̤̍ ga̍ng-chah guâ sing-kô, ló̤-le̤̍h, go̍-cheô seo̍h-má ceh-bī dṳ̂.
43 La̍h-bang i̍ng Ngâ-go̤h gô̤ng, Ca̤̍ sîng-ná̤u-giô̤ⁿ sī guâ ē sîng-ná̤u-giô̤ⁿ, ca̤̍ giô̤ⁿ sī guâ ē giô̤ⁿ, ca̤̍ á̤uⁿ-dīng sī guâ ē á̤uⁿ-dīng, háng dṳ̂ sê̤ kua̍ⁿ-gi̍ng ē mo̍ih-nō̤, deo sī se̤̍h leo̍h guâ: da guâ sîng-ná̤u-giô̤ⁿ léng i sê̤ saⁿ ē giô̤ⁿ, guâ ā̤ ta̤̍ i seh-na ceo̍ lē?
44 N̄g-cṳ́ guâ gah dṳ̂ cá̤ li̍h-io̤h; ceo̍ dṳ̂ guâ do̤-ng ē bíng-gṳ̍.
45 Ngâ-go̤h cuh ta̤̍h sa̤u̍h kio̤̍-kî ceo̍ tiū.
46 Cuh gah ciô̤-gá̤ hiaⁿ-dī gô̤ng, Dṳ̂ kṳ̍ kah sa̤u̍h. Go̤h-náng cuh tá̤h sa̤u̍h to̤̍h siáⁿ seo̍h-dui; cá̤ sia̍h leo̍h sa̤u̍h-dui dêng-ni̍.
47 La̍h-bang heō ca̤̍ sa̤u̍h-dui ga̤̍u leō Î-gia-cî-sah-la̍h-dō-taⁿ. GÎ-gia-cî-sah-la̍h-dō-taⁿ, sī A̍-láng ē uā, huang-lh cuh sī ta̤̍h sa̤u̍h-dui ceo̍ ci̍ng-ge̍ng ē i̍-gī,.) nō̤ Ngâ-go̤h heō i ga̤̍u Gia-lūi-deh. (Gia-lūi-deh sī Hi-beh-lái ē uā, huang-̍h cuh sī ta̤̍h sa̤u̍h-dui ceo̍ ci̍ng-ge̍ng ē i̍-gī.)
48 La̍h-bang gô̤ng, Ging-nua̍ ca̤̍ sa̤u̍h-dui deó dṳ̂ guâ do̤-ng eeo̍ bíng-gṳ̍. Go̍-cheô miá ca̤̍ ga̤̍u Gia-lūi-deh.
49 Buh miá ga̤̍u Bî-seō-ba, (Bî-seō-ba, huang-i̍h cuh sī ga̍ng-chah ē i̍-gī) i̍-seo̍ gô̤ng, Guâ-go̤h-náng seo-lī sí-cā̤, Á̤-Hó̤-Hua dih-dih ga̍ng-chah leo̍h dṳ̂ guâ do̤-ng.
50 Dṳ̂ seh-seô ū hū-bōi guâ sîng-ná̤u-giô̤ⁿ, līng-gōi ca̍i leô beh gá̤, sui-céng beó náng Gua̍ⁿ-gi̍ng dṳ̂ guâ sê̤ ceo̍ ē, nō̤ Siō̤ng-Da̤̍ dih-dih ga̍ng-chah ca̤̍ seō.
51 La̍h-bang buh gah Ngâ-go̤h gô̤ng, Tang kua̍ⁿ dṳ̂ guâ do̤-ng sê̤ to̤̍h ē sa̤u̍h-dui, sê̤ kio̤̍ ē sa̤u̍h-tiū.
52 Ca̤̍ sa̤u̍h-dui ā̤ ceo̍ dih ci̍ng-gṳ̍, ca̤̍ tiū iā ā̤ ceo̍ dih ci̍ng-gṳ̍; guâ n̄g geo̍ ca̤̍ sa̤u̍h-dui hāi dṳ̂, dṳ̂ iā beó geo̍ ca̤̍ dui ca̤̍ tiū hāi guâ.
53 Ngōi A̍-beh-la̍h-hâng sê̤ ceong-gi̍ng ē Siō̤ng-Da̤̍, Ná-ho̤̍h sê̤ ceong-gi̍ng ē Siō̤ng-Da̤̍, léng i cô-co̤ng sê̤ ceong-gi̍ng ē Siō̤ng-Da̤̍, deó dṳ̂ guâ do̤-ng pua̍ng-dua̍ng. Ngâ-go̤h cuh cî i ló̤-bō̤ Î-sah sê̤ gi̍ng-u̍i ē Siō̤ng-Da̤̍ huah-sā̤.
54 Ngâ-go̤h deó suaⁿ lē he̤̍ng-ca̤̍, ca̤u ciô̤-gá̤ hiaⁿ-dī sia̍h e̍ng seo-hōi: sia̍h e̍ng āu, do̍-má deó suaⁿ lē.
55 Dā̤-dī cô̤, La̍h-bang bā-kî, cing náng-dṳ̂-giô̤ⁿ ē chu̍i, ta̤̍ i ce̤h-ho̤h, cuh hói-táu ga̍u bôiⁿ-dā̤-ho̤ng.
## 32
1 Nuâ-go̤h buh kî-sing giáⁿ leo̍h dō-táu, Siō̤ng-Da̤̍ ē seo̍-ciâ gṳ̄-da̤u̍h i.
2 Ngâ-go̤h kua̍ⁿ-gi̍ng i, cuh gô̤ng, Ca̤̍ sī Siō̤ng-Da̤̍ ē geong-bing: go̍-cheô ching ca̤̍ dā̤ ga̤̍ Mâ-ha̍h-niāng. (Mâ-ha̍h-niāng, huang-i̍h cuh sī nn̄g cô geong-bing ē i̍-gī)
3 Ngâ-go̤h cuh sâi náng sa̤ⁿ kṳ̍ Sa̤-cî dā̤ Î-do̤ng chéng lē. gi̍ng i-hiaⁿ Î-sô̤.
4 Heong-nu̍ gô̤ng. Dṳ̂ da̤u̍h du̍i guâ cṳ̂ I-sô̤ gô̤ng, Dṳ̂ ē nó-cái Ngâ-go̤h ah-na gô̤ng, Guâ ceo̍ náng-kā leo̍h La̍h-bang cho̍ lē ga̍u da,
5 ū gú, á̤uⁿ, lṳ́, nó-cái a-táu; de̍h-de̍h sâi náng gah guâ cṳ̂ gô̤ng, n̍g deh ṳng leo̍h guâ cṳ̂ mi̍ng-sá̤ⁿ.
6 Sê̤ sâi ē náng hói-táu, gi̍ng Ngâ-go̤h gô̤ng, Guâ-go̤h-náng gi̍ng dṳ̂-hiaⁿ I-sô̤; i da dua̍ si̍-bā náng lí cī dṳ̂.
7 Ngâ-go̤h sing lē giaⁿ cīng tiâng; cio̤ng i gṳng-súi ē náng, léng gú, á̤uⁿ, lo̤̍h-dó̤, beong ceo̍ nn̄g dūi:
8 gô̤ng, Seh-seô Î-sô̤ lí pā ca̤̍ seo̍h dūi, ho̤h ū la̍u-gau seo̍h dūi ā̤ câu kṳ̍.
9 Ngâ-go̤h cuh gí-dô̤ gô̤ng, Guâ go̤ng A̍-beh-la̍h-hâng ē Siō̤ng-Da̤̍, Guâ ló̤-bō̤ Î-sah ē Siō̤ng-Da̤̍ Á̤-Hó̤-Hua ā. Dṳ̂ sá̤ⁿ-di̍h mīng Guâ gô̤ng, Da̤u̍h hói-táu kṳ̍ bôiⁿ-dā̤ bôiⁿ-ceo̤̍h, A-Sa̤̍-Gi̍.u: no. Guâ dih-dih si-ṳng keoh dṳ̂:
10 Cṳ̂ sê̤ si keoh nó-cái ē ṳng-hā̤ gah cing-si̍h, cuh nō̤ da̍i sa̤̍ ē, guâ iā n̄g-tang siū; guâ sá̤ⁿ-di̍h nō̤ ta̤̍h seo̍h gi tā̤uⁿ geo̍ ca̤̍ Io̤h-dāng-eó, da sâi guâ do̍i-giáⁿ ē náng î-ging siáⁿ ceo̍ nn̄g dūi.
11 Guâ u̍i guâ-hiaⁿ Î-sô̤; giaⁿ i ā̤ lí pā guâ, léng guâ bô-giô̤ⁿ, go̍-cheô giú Cṳ̂ giu̍ guâ, tuah-lī i ē chiû.
12 Cṳ̂ beh-céng ṳ̂ng guâ gô̤ng, Guâ dih-dih si-ṳng keoh dṳ̂, sâi dṳ̂ hā̤u-a̤̍ chā̤uⁿ hâi-sua hiô̤-sā̤, bā̤-sua̍ⁿ-dih lâ̤u.
13 Ngâ-go̤h do̍-má leo̍h hṳ̂-hiô̤. iú i sê̤ ū ē mo̍ih-nō̤ gâ̤ⁿ ā lâ̤-beo̍h, sa̍ng keoh i-hiaⁿ Î-sô̤:
14 beô ē. suaⁿ-á̤uⁿ nn̄g-bā táu, gang ē dī-se̍h táu; beô ē míng-á̤uⁿ nn̄g-bā táu, gang ē dī-se̍h táu;
15 beô ē lo̤̍h-dó̤ léng sia̍h neng ē lo̤̍h-dó̤-giô̤ⁿ so̤ⁿ. se̍h táu, beô ē gú si̍-se̍h táu, gang ē se̍h táu; beô ē lṳ́ dī-se̍h táu, lṳ́-giô̤ⁿ se̍h táu.
16 Bôiⁿ-lūi gṳ̄-go̤h ceo̍ seo̍h dīng, gau i nó-cái ē chiû, gah i gô̤ng, Dṳ̂ ceo̍-sa̤ⁿ guâ kṳ̍, sâi go̤h-dīng seo lī.
17 Heong-nu̍ táu-sá̤ⁿ ē nó-cái, gô̤ng, Guâ hiaⁿ Î-sô̤ gṳ̄-dau̍h dṳ̂, ka̍ng dṳ̂ sī h̍g-náng? beoh kṳ̍ deoh-leo? dṳ̂ sê̤ de̤̍h ē táu-sa sī hn̍g-náng ē?
18 Dṳ̂ da̤u̍h i̍ng gô̤ng, Sī se̤̍h guâ cṳ̂ ē nó-cái Ngâ-go̤h ē, a̍i-dí sa̍ng guâ cṳ̂ Î-sô̤ ceo̍ lâ̤-beo̍h, Ngâ-go̤h iā deó guâ āu-li̍.
19 Buh heong-nu̍ dā̤-dī bang, dā̤-so̤ⁿ bang, léng ló̤ng-cô̤ng de̤̍h go̤h-dīng táu-saⁿ ē náng, gô̤ng, Dṳ̂ gṳ̄-da̤u̍h Î-sô̤ cuh da̤u̍h ah-na gah i gô̤ng;
20 buh da̤u̍h gô̤ng, Dṳ̂ nó-cái Ngâ-go̤h deó guâ āu-li̍. Ing Ngâ-go̤h siô̤ng gô̤ng, Guâ ceo̍-sa̤ⁿ sa̍ng lâ̤-beo̍h leo̍h ā̤-hiaⁿ, sâi ā-hiaⁿ ca̍i hó̤-bo̤̍h, do̍i-āu gah i gi̍ng-mi̍ng; he̍h-chiâⁿ i ā̤ sṳ̍ Sû: I.ng-Sa̤̍-Gi̍. gâ.
21 Bíng ah-na, lâ̤-beo̍h sa̤ⁿ) kṳ-kêng bu̍; cî seo̍h-má, Ngâ-go̤h deó dūi I geh ga̍ lē do̤-ng do̍-ńá-n- go̤ se I ta̤̍h nn̄g
22 Léng-má bā-kî, sâi nn̄g gá̤ ba̍ng leo̍ lāu-mâ, nn̄g gá̤ a-táu, léng se̍h-ih giô̤ⁿ deó gá̤ giô̤ⁿ, geo̍ Ngâ-bo̤̍h ka̤ ē seóng-seh deó dō.
23 Sâi ga-se̤̍h geo̍ ca̤̍ eó, cuh I sá̤ⁿ, pah iā sâi ló̤ng-cô̤ng ē mo̍ih-nō̤ deo geo̍ gah i-hia kṳ̍.
24 Nō̤ sīng Ngâ-go̤h duaⁿ-gá̤ I geo̍ cī i, deó hiô̤; ū seo̍h gá̤ náng gah i da̍u-dāu-lṳ ci li̍h, di̍h-táu ga̍u ting beoh gng.
25 I hâu. ē Ca̤̍ náng gi̍ng ga̍i bā̤ iô̤ⁿ Ngâ-go̤h, sîng-ná̤u-cuh pā Ngâ-go̤h ē cûi-táu; Ngâ-gô̤ng, Ca̤ go̤h gah i da̍u-lih sí-cā̤, tûi-geoh Ngâ-go̤h teo̍h-leóng kṳ̍.
26 Ca̤̍ náng gô̤ng. Siō̤ng-D Ting beoh gng leō, chiâⁿ ba̍ng guâ I ē. N kṳ̍. Ngâ-go̤h gô̤ng. Dṳ̂ nō̤ n̄g ta̤̍ cuh giáⁿ guâ ce̤h-ho̤h,
27 guâ dih-dih n̄g ba̍ng I ē giô̤ⁿ, iā dṳ̂ kṳ̍.
28 I cuh ka̍ng gô̤ng, Dṳ̂ seh gah miá seo̍h-moi: i̍ng gô̤ng, Ngâ-go̤h. Î-sô̤ gô̤h
29 I gô̤ng, Da í-hā̤u, dṳ̂ miá beó i seo̍h geóh ca̍i ga̤̍u Ngâ-go̤h, nō̤ miá ga̤̍u Î-seo̍h-moi seh-le̍h-ing dṳ̂ gah Siō̤ng-Da̤̍ léng I
30 mó̤ng gu náng da̍u-li̍h, deo ā̤ deh-si̍ng.!heoh guâ Ngâ-go̤h ka̍ng i gô̤ng, Chiâⁿ ta̤̍h! dī ā, guâ dṳ̂ ē miá gah guâ gô̤ng? i̍ng gô̤ng, I ē, cíng-cé Seh-na ka̍ng guâ ē miá lē? Cuh I go̤h gô̤ng, deó hiô̤ ta̤̍ Ngâ-go̤h ce̤h-ho̤h.
31 seh-seô co̍ Ngâ-go̤h ching ca̤̍ dā̤-ho̤ng ē miá chiâⁿ dṳ̂ i ga̤̍u Bī-nó-i-leh, Gī-nó-i-leh I beo̍h; ing huang-i̍h cuh sī gi̍ng-mi̍ng Siō̤ng- chā̤uⁿ gi̍g Da̤̍ ē i̍-gī) gô̤ng, Sui-céng gah ā̤uⁿ, léng Siō̤ng-Da̤̍ gi̍ng mi̍ng, guâ ē ua̍h-2 Siō̤ng-miā ho̤h diō̤.
32 Ngâ-go̤h geo̍ Bī-guâ da̍i ci̍ nó-i-leh sí-cā̤, di̍h-táu no̤̍ cheoh, sê̤ sa̍ng ē ko̤-dô siū sio̤ng, ko-ko̤-giáⁿ. Ing Iō̤, do̍i-āu ca̤̍ náng pā Ngâ-go̤h ē ko̤-dô, Guâ-go̤h-sio̤ng i ē duā-gṳng, go̍-cheô Î-seh-I giáⁿ, guâ le̍h náng beó sia̍h ko̤ dô ē duā13 Ngâ-go̤ gṳng, ga̍u ging-nua̍-di̍h. cai ciô̤-gá̤
## 33
1 tá̤uⁿ, no̤̍ Ngâ-go̤h gio̤̍h-ki ma̍h-ciu, gi̍ng I gíng de̤̍h Î-sô̤ dua̍ si̍-bā náng lí, cuh I dīng táu-sê ciô̤-sā̤ giô̤ⁿ, sâi Lī-a̍, La̍h-nn̄g
2 gá̤ a-táu chio̤̍ i, gá̤ a-táu léng i ē giô̤ⁿ táu-sá̤ⁿ, Lī-a̍ léng i ē co̤-ng, La̍h-geh gah Io̤h-bo̍i-āu.
3 Ga̍i cuh giáⁿ-leo̍h dā̤ lē chih hói, cia̍ⁿ seo gṳ̄ng.
4 Î-sô̤ câu beō i leo̍h ho̤ng-sá̤ⁿ, gau -chu̍i; nn̄g gá̤ náng cá̤ sô̤ gio̤̍h-kî ma̍h-ciu, gi̍ng náng
5 léng da̍i-giô̤ⁿ; ka̍ng gah dṳ̂ cá̤ ē sī hn̍g-náng? ng gô̤ng, Ca̤̍ ciô̤-gá̤ giô̤ⁿ si-ṳng seo̍ keoh nó-cái ēg gá̤ a-táu léng i giô̤ⁿ se̤̍ⁿ ba̍i i.
6 
7 Lī-a̍ léng i giáⁿ sá̤ⁿ ba̍i i: î-hā̤u Io̤h-d̍h-geh iā giáⁿ sá̤ⁿ ba̍i i., Guâ sê̤ gṳ̄-da̤u̍h ē
8 ng táu-saⁿ, dṳ̂ beoh ceo̍ sâi? I̍g gô̤ng, Ai-dí â cṳ̂ ē ṳng, hō̤ng sa̍ng cṳ̂.
9 Î-sô̤ gô̤ng, Guâ che̤ng-ce̤h lâ̤u; dṳ̂ sê̤ ū ng gui leo̍h dṳ̂.
10 Ngâ-Chiâⁿ beó ngā̤ te̤-sí; guâ hṳng leo̍h dṳ̂ mi̍ng-sá̤ⁿ, guâ chiû lē siu ca̤̍ lâ̤-guâ gi̍ng dṳ̂ ē mi̍ng, Siō̤ng-Da̤̍ ē mi̍ng seo̍h-mó̤ng dṳ̂
11 huaⁿ-hî guâ. la̤̍ si-ṳng keoh guâ, sâi e̤ng-ce̤h; chiâⁿ siu guâ â̤-beo̍h. Ngê̤-go̤h ngā̤-a̍ⁿ siu.
12 Î-sô̤ gô̤ng, náng meô kî-sing cá̤ eo̍h dṳ̂ táu-sá̤ⁿ chio̤̍-dō. i̍ng gô̤ng, Guâ cṳ̂
13 ā̤ da̍i-giô̤ⁿ sī nôi-cio̤̍h, gú, a̤u-giô̤ⁿ: seh-seô gíng-giáⁿ seo̍h di̍h, ca̤̍ seo̍h-deo ā̤ sî.
14 Chiâ guâ cṳ̂ ceo̍-sa̤ⁿ nó-cái giáⁿ kṳ̍: nó-cái da̤u̍h liō̤ng táu-saⁿ gah sa̤̍-giô̤ⁿ ē li̍h, mā̤-mā̤ giáⁿ, dêng nó-cái ga̍u guâ cṳ̂ ē sê̤-cāi Sa̤-cî dā̤-ho̤ng.
15 Î-sô̤ gô̤ng. Ê̤ng guâ láu gṳng-súi ē náng gûi gá̤, gah dṳ̂ cá̤ giáⁿ. Ngâ-go̤h i̍ng gô̤ng, Deoh-nâi ah-na? nō̤ ngōi deh ṳng leo̍h guâ cṳ̂ cuh ce̤h-I̍.
16 Cî seo̍h di̍h. Î-sô̤ seóng-dō po̤ⁿ-dôiⁿ kṳ̍ Sa̤-cî.
17 Ngâ-go̤h giáⁿ ga̍u So̍-gah, ta̤̍ ga̍ kî cho̍, iā ta̤̍ táu-saⁿ dō̤ châu-lá̤u go̍-cheô miá ca̤̍ dā̤-ho̤ng ga̤̍u So̍-gah. (So̍-gah, huang-ih cuh sī châu-lá̤u ē i̍-gī.)
18 Ngâ-go̤h iu Ba-tâng-a̍-láng hói-táu sí-cā̤, cuh bíng-ang ga̍u Gia-náng dā̤, Sī-gia̍ng ē gā̤ⁿ-siáⁿ.
19 Deó siáⁿ táu-sá̤ⁿ dō̤ da̤̍uⁿ-báng; i-ha̤u ta̤̍h seo̍h-bā ngṳ́ng gah Sī-gia̍ng ē ló̤-bō̤ Ha̍h-bua̍h ē giô̤ⁿ-seong bâ̤ seo̍h po̍i chéng, cuh sī dō̤ da̤̍uⁿ-báng ē ūi-chṳ̍.
20 Deó hiô̤ de̤h ca̤̍-dái, ching i ē miá ga̤̍u Î-seh-le̍h Siō̤ng-Da̤̍ ē ca̤̍-dái.

# Cheoh-Ai-gi̍h

# Lī-bī Gi̍

# Míng-so̍ Gi̍

# Sing-mīng Gi̍

# Io̤h-sṳ-a̍ Cṳ

# Seō-seo Gi̍

# Lō-deh Gi̍

# Sah-bâ̤u-cî Céng-cṳ

# Sah-bâ̤u-cî Hā̤u-cṳ

# Le̍h-ó̤ng Siō̤ng-ge̤̍ng

# Le̍h-ó̤ng Hā-ge̤̍ng

# Le̍h-dāi Siō̤ng-ge̤̍ng
## 1
1 A̍-do̤ng, Seh, Î-nâ-seō,
2 Gai-náng, Mâ-leh-le̍h, Á̤-le̍h,
3 Î-lo̤̍h, Mâ-tô-sah-la̍h, La̍h-be̍h,
4 Nô̤-a̍, Siâng, Háng, Ngâ-heo̍h.
5 Ngâ-heo̍h ē giô̤ⁿ sī Ko̤-be̍h, Mâ-go̤h Mâ-ta̍i, Ngâ-huang, Tô-bah, Bî-seh, Dá̤-la̍h.
6 Ko̤-be̍h ē giô̤ⁿ sī A̍-si̍h-gi-ná, Lî-hah, Do̤-gia-mâ.
7 Ngâ-huang ē giô̤ⁿ sī I-lī-sa, Dāi-sih, Gi-dâ̤, Do̤-dang.
8 Háng ē giô̤ⁿ sī Gô-si̍h, Be̍h-sa̤, Heo̍h, Gia-náng.
9 Gô-si̍h ē giô̤ⁿ sī Sa̤-ba, Ha̍h-hi-la̍h, Seh-dāi, La̍h-mâ, Seh-dá̤-gia. La̍h-mâ ē giô̤ⁿ sī Sī-ba, Dâ̤-dāng.
10 Gô-si̍h saⁿ Néng-le̤̍h; i sī ing-hé̤ng ga̍i-sa̤̍.
11 Be̍h-sa̤ saⁿ Lō-deng co̤̍h, Ang-náng co̤̍h, Lī-ha̍h co̤̍h, Da̍h-tô co̤̍h,
12 Bah-lô co̤̍h, Gah-lō co̤̍h, Gah-to̤h co̤̍h, (Gah-lō saⁿ Hi-lī-seō co̤̍h.)
13 Gia-náng saⁿ diô̤ng-ceô Sa̤-deo̍ng, buh saⁿ Heh co̤̍h,
14 buh saⁿ Á̤-bo̍-seō co̤̍h, A̍-mó̤-lî co̤̍h, Geh-gia-sa̤ co̤̍h,
15 Hi-bī co̤̍h, A̍-gi co̤̍h, Sa̤-ní co̤̍h,
16 A̍-uâ-dâ̤ co̤̍h, Sêng-mâ-lî co̤̍h, Ha̍h-bua̍h co̤̍h.
17 Siâng ē giô̤ⁿ sī Î-láng, A̍-seo̍h, A̍-hah-sah, Lō-deh, A̍-láng, O-seō, Hō-leh, Gí-tiah, Bî-seh,
18 A̍-hah-sah saⁿ Sī-la̍h, Sī-la̍h saⁿ Hi-beh.
19 Hi-beh saⁿ nn̄g gá̤ giô̤ⁿ: seo̍h gá̤ miá Hah-leh; ing do̤ng-sí sa̤̍-sā̤uⁿ ē náng beong kui; i-dī miá Io̤h-dang.
20 Io̤h-dang saⁿ A̍-mó̤-da̍h, Sa-la̍h, Hah-sah-mâ-hi, Á̤-la̍h,
21 Ha̍h-do̤-láng, O-sah, De̍h-la̍h,
22 I-bah, A̍-bi̍-mâ-lī, Sī-ba,
23 O̤-hi, Ha̍h-hi-la̍h, Io̤h-bah. Ca̤̍ deo sī Io̤h-dang ē giô̤ⁿ.
24 Siâng, A̍-hah-sah, Sī-la̍h,
25 Hi-beh, Hah-leh, La̍h-gâ̤u,
26 Sah-lo̤̍h, Ná-ho̤̍h, Taⁿ-la̍h,
27 A̍-beh-láng, (A̍-beh-láng cuh sī A̍-beh-la̍h-hâng.)
28 A̍-beh-la̍h-hâng ē giô̤ⁿ: sī Î-sah gah I-si̍h-mâ-lī.
29 Î-sah gah I-si̍h-mâ-lī ē hā̤u-a̤̍ gi̍ leo̍h ō̤-dâ̤: I-si̍h-mâ-lī ē diô̤ng-ceô sī Ní-bái-io̤h; buh saⁿ Gi-da̍h, Ah-be̍h, Bi̍h-sang,
30 Bi̍h-mâ, Tô-mâ, Mâ-sah, Ha̍h-da̍h, Dá̤-mâ,
31 Ih-do̤h, Ná-hi-si̍h, Gí-dâ̤-mâ. Ca̤̍ deo sī I-si̍h-mâ-lī ē giô̤ⁿ.
32 A̍-beh-la̍h-hâng ē a-lî Gi-do̤-la̍h sê̤ saⁿ ē giô̤ⁿ: cuh sī Sing-láng, Io̤h-sang, Bî-dāng, Bî-déng, Ih-bah, Sṳ-a̍. Io̤h-sang ē giô̤ⁿ sī Sī-ba gah Dâ̤-dāng.
33 Bî-déng ē giô̤ⁿ sī Î-huah, I-heo̍h, Ha̍h-do̤̍h, A-bi̍-dāi, I-leh-dāi. Ca̤̍ deo sī Gi-do-la̍h ē giô̤ⁿ.
34 A̍-beh-la̍h-hâng saⁿ Î-sah, Î-sah ē giô̤ⁿ sī Î-sô̤ gah Î-seh-le̍h.
35 Î-sô̤ ē giô̤ⁿ sī Î-lī-ha̍h, Liú-cî, Á̤-sī, Ngâ-láng, Kô̤-la̍h.
36 Î-lī-ha̍h ē giô̤ⁿ sī Dá̤-māng, O̤-buah, Sa̤-bo̤, Gia-dāng, Gi-da̍h, Déng-da̍h, A̍-mâ-li̍h.
37 Liú-cî ē giô̤ⁿ sī Ná-ha̍h, Sa̤-la̍h, Sa-mâ, Bî-sah.
38 Sa̤-cî ē giô̤ⁿ sī Ló̤-dang, Se̤h-bah, Ca̤̍-bēng, A̍-ná, Dâ̤-seōng, Î-chah, Dâ̤-sang.
39 Ló̤-dang ē giô̤ⁿ sī Hó̤-lī, Hi-māng; Ló̤-dang ē ā-mo̍i sī Déng-da̍h.
40 Se̤h-bah ē giô̤ⁿ sī A̍-leh-meóng, Mâ-ná-ha̍h, I-bah, Sī-bo̤, O̤-náng. Ca̤̍-bēng ē giô̤ⁿ sī A̍-ngâ gah A̍-ná.
41 A̍-ná ē giô̤ⁿ sī Dâ̤-seōng. Dâ̤-seōng ē giô̤ⁿ sī Ha̍h-beh-láng, Ih-sī-bang, Ih-láng, Gi-láng.
42 Î-chah ē giô̤ⁿ sī Pih-hâng, Sah-huang, Á̤-gang. Dâ̤-sang ē giô̤ⁿ sī O-seō gah A̍-láng.
43 Î-seh-le̍h náng bōi ū ó̤ng guâng-lî î-céng, Î-do̤ng dā̤-ho̤ng ū le̍h-ó̤ng guâng-dī, i ē miá gi̍ leo̍h ō̤-dâ̤: Bi̍-cî ē giô̤ⁿ Bi̍-la̍h ceo̍ ó̤ng; i ging-siáⁿ miá Déng-ha̍h-ba.
44 Bi̍-la̍h sî āu, Po̤̍-seo-la̍h náng Sa̤-la̍h ē giô̤ⁿ Io̤h-bah ciah-ūi.
45 Io̤h-bah sî āu, Dá̤-māng náng Hō-sang ciah-ūi.
46 Hō-sang sī āu, Bi̍-tah ē giô̤ⁿ Ha̍h-tah ciah-ūi, Ha̍h-tah beh-céng leo̍h Mó̤-ah ē báⁿ-á̤uⁿ pā Bî-déng náng; i ging-siáⁿ miá A̍-bī-deh.
47 Ha̍h-tah sî āu, Mâ-seo-lî-gia náng Sang-la̍h ciah-ūi.
48 Sang-la̍h sî āu, eó-bing ē Lî-hó̤-beh náng Sô̤-ló̤ ciah-ūi.
49 Sô̤-ló̤ sî āu, A-geh-po̤̍ ē giô̤ⁿ Ba-leh-ha̍h-náng ciah-ūi.
50 Ba-leh-ha̍h-náng sî āu, Ha̍h-da̍h ciah-ūi; i ging-siáⁿ miá Bô̤: i ó̤ng-hā̤u miá Bî-hi-dāi-beh, sī Mâ-de̍h-le̍h ē sîng-ná̤u-giô̤ⁿ, Bî-sah-ha̍h ē sîng-ná̤u-seong.
51 Ha̍h-da̍h sî kṳ̍. Î-do̤ng co̤̍h ū ca̤̍ co̤̍h-dâ̤uⁿ cuh sī Déng-ná co̤̍h-dâ̤uⁿ, A-leh-uâ co̤̍h-dâ̤uⁿ, Á̤-tiah co̤̍h-dâ̤uⁿ,
52 A̍-hó̤-lî-ba-mâ co̤̍h-dâ̤uⁿ, Î-la̍h co̤̍h-dâ̤uⁿ, Bî-neōng co̤̍h-dâ̤uⁿ,
53 Gi-da̍h co̤̍h-dâ̤uⁿ, Dá̤-māng co̤̍h-dâ̤uⁿ, Bi̍h-sah co̤̍h-dâ̤uⁿ,
54 Be̍h-teh co̤̍h-dâ̤uⁿ, Î-láng co̤̍h-dâ̤uⁿ. Ca̤̍ deo sī Î-do̤ng ē co̤̍h-dâ̤uⁿ.
## 2
1 Î-seh-le̍h ē giô̤ⁿ sī Liú-bēng, Sa̤-me̍ng, Lī-bī, Iú-dāi, Î-sah-gia, Sa̤-bo̍-leóng,
2 Dāng, Io̤h-seh, Bēng-ngâ-mîng, Da̍h-dāi-lī, Gia-deh, A̍-seh.
3 Iú-dāi ē giô̤ⁿ sī Cî, O̤-náng, Sī-la̍h; ca̤̍ so̤ⁿ gá̤ sī Gia-náng náng Sṳ-a̍ ē sîng-ná̤u-giô̤ⁿ sê̤ saⁿ ē. Iú-dāi ē duā-giô̤ⁿ Cî ā héng-o̤h leo̍h Á̤-Hó̤-Hua mi̍ng-sá̤ⁿ; Á̤-Hó̤-Hua cuh tái i.
4 Iú-dāi gah i sîng-mū Dāi-mâ saⁿ Hah-leh-seō gah Sa̤-la̍h, Iú-dāi gē̤ng-cô̤ng ngō gá̤ giô̤ⁿ.
5 Hah-leh-seō ē giô̤ⁿ sī Hi-seō-leóng, Ha̍h-bâ̤u-leh.
6 Sa̤-la̍h ē giô̤ⁿ sī Sing-lî, Î-ta̍ng, Hi-māng, Gah-go̤h, Da̍h-dāi; gē̤ng-cô̤ng ngō gá̤ giô̤ⁿ.
7 Gia-bî ē giô̤ⁿ sī A̍-gang, beh-céng tau-ta̤̍h ca̤̍ he̤̍ng keoh Á̤-Hó̤-Hua ē mo̍ih-nō̤, sâi Î-seh-le̍h náng siū cai-hō̤, cuh sī i.
8 Î-ta̍ng ē giô̤ⁿ sī A̍-sah-lî-a̍.
9 Hi-seō-leóng sê̤ saⁿ ē giô̤ⁿ sī Á̤-la̍h-be̍h, Láng, Gí-lō-ba̍i.
10 Láng saⁿ A̍-bî-ná-da̍h, A̍-bî-ná-da̍h saⁿ Iú-dāi co̤̍h ē bo̤̍h-beh Ná-seōng;
11 Ná-seōng saⁿ Sah-meóng, Sah-meóng saⁿ Bo̤-seō,
12 Bo̤-seō saⁿ O̤-beh, O̤-beh saⁿ Á̤-sa̤;
13 Á̤-sa̤ saⁿ diô̤ng-ceô Î-lī-ah, dā̤-dī A̍-bi̍-ná-tah, dā̤-so̤ⁿ Sī-bôi,
14 dā̤-si̍ Ná-dāng-gia̍h, dā̤-ngō La̍h-ta̍i,
15 dā̤-la̍h Ō̤-seng, dā̤-chih Dāi-bi̍h,
16 i-go̤h-náng ē cî-mo̍i sī Sa̤-lô-ngâ, A̍-bi̍-gai. Sa̤-lô-ngâ saⁿ so̤ⁿ gá̤ giô̤ⁿ, A̍-bi̍-seo, Io̤h-ah, A̍-sah-heh.
17 A̍-bi̍-gai saⁿ A̍-mâ-seh; A̍-mâ-seh ē ló̤-bō̤ sī I-si̍h-mâ-lī náng Ih-tiah.
18 Hi-seō-leóng ē giô̤ⁿ Gia-leh teô A̍-so-ba gah Á̤-lio̤̍h ceo̍ lāu-mâ saⁿ giô̤ⁿ; A̍-so-ba ē giô̤ⁿ sī Á̤-seh, Se̤h-ba, Ah-deong.
19 A̍-so-ba sî āu, Gia-leh buh teô Î-hah-dāi ceo̍ lāu-mâ, cuh saⁿ Hō-cî.
20 Hō-cî saⁿ O-lī, O-lī saⁿ Bi̍-sah-le̍h.
21 Î-hā̤u Hi-seō-leóng la̍h-se̍h ho̍i sí-cā̤, teô Gi-le̍h ē ló̤-bō̤ Mâ-gih ē sîng-ná̤u-giô̤ⁿ; gah i saⁿ Sa̤-gah.
22 Sa̤-gah saⁿ Gá-cî, Gá-cî leo̍h Gi-le̍h dā̤-ho̤ng ū dī-se̍h-so̤ⁿ ceō siáⁿ.
23 Î-hā̤u Gi-seo̍h náng gah A̍-láng náng dua̍h kṳ̍ Gá-cî go̤h ceō ē siáⁿ, léng Gi-da̍h, gah se̤̍h Gi-da̍h la̍h-se̍h ceō siáⁿ. Sa̤-gah gah Gá-cî nn̄g co̤̍h deo sī Gi-le̍h ē ló̤-bō̤ Mâ-gih ē giô̤ⁿ-seong.
24 Hi-seō-leóng leo̍h Î-hah-dāi Gia-leh sî āu, I ē lāu-mâ A̍-bî-a̍ gah i saⁿ A̍-seo̍h, A̍-seo̍h sī Dá̤-go̤-a̍ ē ló̤-bō̤.
25 Hi-seō-leóng ē diô̤ng-ceô Á̤-la̍h-be̍h ē diô̤ng-ceô Sī-láng, buh saⁿ Bo̍-ná, O̤-léng, O̤-seng, A̍-hi-ngâ.
26 Á̤-la̍h-be̍hbuh teô ā lāu-mâ miá A̍-taⁿ-la̍h, i sī O̤-náng ē ná̤u-lâ̤.
27 Á̤-la̍h-be̍h ē diô̤ng-ceô Láng ē giô̤ⁿ sī Mâ-seō, Ngâ-míng, Î-geh.
28 O̤-náng ē giô̤ⁿ sī Sa-mâi gah Ngâ-taⁿ: Sa-mâi ē giô̤ⁿ sī Ná-dah gah A̍-bi̍-seo̍h.
29 A̍-bi̍-seo̍h ē lāu-mâ miá A̍-bi̍-hái; gah i saⁿ A̍-bēng gah Mó̤-li̍h.
30 Ná-dah ē giô̤ⁿ sī Sa̤-leh gah A̍-be̍ng, Sa̤-leh bōi saⁿ giô̤ⁿ cuh sî kṳ̍.
31 A̍-be̍ng ē giô̤ⁿ sī Î-sī. Î-sī ē giô̤ⁿ sī Sī-sang: Sī-sang ē giô̤ⁿ sī A̍-lái.
32 Sa-mâi ē ā-dī Ngâ-taⁿ, i ē giô̤ⁿ sī Ih-tiah gah Io̤h-ná-dang; Ih-tiah bōi saⁿ giô̤ⁿ cuh sî kṳ̍.
32 Io̤h-ná-dang ē giô̤ⁿ sī Bî-leh gah Sah-sah. Ca̤̍ deo sī Á̤-la̍h-be̍h ē giô̤ⁿ seong.
34 Sī-sang beó dah-bo-giô̤ⁿ, nō̤ ū sîng-ná̤u-giô̤ⁿ. Sī-sang ū ā nó-cái Ai-gi̍h náng miá Á̤-la̍h.
35 Sī-sang cio̤ng i sîng-ná̤u-giô̤ⁿ po̍i i nó-cái Á̤-la̍h ceo̍ lāu-mâ; gah i saⁿ A̍-ta̍i.
36 A̍-ta̍i saⁿ Ná-dang, Ná-dang saⁿ Sah-bah,
37 Sah-bah saⁿ I-heo̍h-la̍h, I-heo̍h-la̍h saⁿ O̤-beh,
38 O̤-beh saⁿ Á̤-hō, Á̤-hō saⁿ A̍-sah-lî-a̍,
39 A̍-sah-lî-a̍ saⁿ Hi-li̍h, Hi-li̍h saⁿ Î-lī-a̍-sah,
40 Î-lī-a̍-sah saⁿ Sa̤-sa-mâi, Sa̤-sa-mâi saⁿ Sa̤-lé̤ng,
41 Sa̤-léng saⁿ Á̤-ga-bî, Á̤-ga-bî saⁿ Î-lī-sa-mâ.
42 Á̤-la̍h-be̍h ē hiaⁿ-dī Gia-leh ē giô̤ⁿ, diô̤ng-ceô sī Bî-sa, Bî-sa sī Sa̤-heo̍h gah Mâ-lī-sa náng ē cô-hū.
43 Hi-beh-leóng ē giô̤ⁿ sī Kô̤-la̍h, Dāi-bo̍-a̍, Lî-ging, Sī-mâ.
44 Sī-mâ saⁿ La̍h-háng, La̍h-háng saⁿ Io̤h-gua̍ng; Lî-ging saⁿ Sa-mâi.
45 Sa-mâi ē giô̤ⁿ sī Mâ-eóng; Mâ-eóng sī Beh-se̤h ē cô-hū.
46 Gia-leh ē a-lî Î-hah, saⁿ Ha̍h-láng, Mó̤-sah, Gia-seh.
47 Ngâ-ta̍i ē giô̤ⁿ sī Lî-gia̍ng, Io̤h-da̍ng, Gî-sang, Bî-li̍h, I-hah, Sa-ah.
48 Gia-leh ē a-lî Mâ-gia saⁿ Sī-be̍h, Deh-ha̍h-ná.
49 Buh saⁿ Beh-mâ-ná ē cô-hū Sa-ah, Buah-bî-ná gah Gi-bi̍-a̍ ē cô-hū Sī-hah; Gia-leh ē sîng-ná̤u-giô̤ⁿ sī Ah-sah.
50 Ca̤̍ deo sī Gia-leh ē giô̤ⁿ-seong: I-hah-dāi ū diô̤ng-ceô, Hō-cî ē giô̤ⁿ, cuh sī Gi-le̍h-á̤-líng ē cô-hū Se̤h-bah,
51 Beh-lī-héng ē cô-hū Sah-mâ, Beh-gia-deh ē cô-hū Ha̍h-leh.
52 Gi-le̍h-á̤-líng ē cô-hū Se̤h-bah ē giô̤ⁿ: cuh sī Ha̍h-ló̤-î, do̍ Mâ-ná-heh, hiô̤ seo̍h-bua̍ⁿ ē náng, sī i ē hā̤u-a̤̍. 
53 Gi-le̍h-á̤-líng ē co̤̍h, cuh sī I-deh náng, Bo̍ náng, Sṳ-mâ náng, Bi̍h-lái náng; buh iú ca̤̍ co̤̍h do̤-ng saⁿ cheoh Sah-li̍h náng, léng Ih-li̍h náng.
54 Sah-mâ ē giô̤ⁿ-seong sī Beh-lī-héng náng, Ní-dó̤-hah náng, A̍-dāi-le̤̍h Beh-io̤h-ah náng, Mâ-ná-heh seo̍h-bua̍ⁿ ē náng, Sê̤-lî náng.
55 Léng do̍ Ngâ-bih hiô̤-sā̤ ta̍h-cṳ náng go̤h cô; cuh sī Deh-la̍h náng. Sī-bî-ah náng, So-ah náng. Ca̤̍ deo sī Gi-ní náng, sī Lî-gah ga ē cô-hū Ha̍h-bua̍h sê̤ dé̤ng-liú ē.

# Le̍h-dāi Hā-ge̤̍ng

# Î-seō-la̍h Cṳ

# Ní-hi-bî Gi̍

# Î-seō-tiah Cṳ

# Io̤h-beh Gi̍

# Si-peng

# Cing-ngé̤ng

# Dé̤ng-dō̤ Cṳ
## 1
1 Á̤-lō-sah-lêng ó̤ng Dāi-bi̍h ē giô̤ⁿ Ko̤-hi-le̍h, (he̍h ceo̍ dé̤ng-dō̤ ē náng, ō̤-dâ̤ iā sī na) i ē uā gi̍ leo̍h ō̤-dâ̤.
2 Ko̤-hi-le̍h gô̤ng, Háng seō hṳ-kang do̤-ng ē hṳ-kang; hṳ-kang do̤-ng ē hṳ-kang, deo sī se̤̍h leo̍h hṳ-kang.
3 Náng diō̤ di̍h-táu ō̤ ló̤-kô ceo̍ gang-hu, ū seo̍h-moi iā lē?
4 Seo̍h dāi geo̍ kṳ̍, seo̍h dāi buh lí; nō̤ ca̤̍ duā dā̤ ṳ̂ng-ê̤ng deo diō̤.
5 Di̍h-táu cheoh, di̍h-táu leo̍h, cīng gîng kuáng ga̍u ga̍i ē sê̤-cāi, buh ca̍i ca̤̍u chōi.
6 Boiⁿ te̍ng náng-ha̤̍uⁿ choi, buh dôiⁿ ga̍u bah-ha̤̍uⁿ; sí-sió̤ng chia-dôiⁿ hiô̤ choi; boiⁿ iú deoh-leo chia-dôiⁿ, buh ā̤ chia-dôiⁿ ga̍u hiô̤ ūi-chṳ̍.
7 Gang-eó deo láu di̍h hâi, hâi iā bā̤ muâ; gang-eó iú deoh-leo láu, buh ā̤ gui hiô̤ ūi-chṳ̍ ca̍i láu.
8 Cio̤̍h-céng ta̤̍h māng-seō dah-de̤̍ng-giô̤ⁿ a̤̍u lí leōng, dih-dih ga̍u duā ló̤-kô; náng gô̤ng iā bā̤ cīng: ma̍h-ciu kua̍ⁿ bā̤ bô̤, hī-kang tiaⁿ bā̤ ce̤h.
9 Î-ging ū lâ̤u ē seō, î-hā̤u dih-dih ca̍i ū; î-ging siáⁿ lâ̤u ē seō, î-hā̤u dih-dih ca̍i siáⁿ, ting-ō̤ bi̍ng beó sing ē seō.
10 Deoh-seo̍h ā̤uⁿ ē seō, náng ū-dang-dng cî, gô̤ng, Ca̤̍ sī sing ē seō le? i ho̤h n̄g-cai ca̤̍ seō diō̤ guâ-go̤h-náng î-céng ē sa̤̍-dāi cuh ū lâ̤u.
11 Î-ô̤ng ē seō, da beó ā̤ gi̍-niāng ē náng, cio̤ng-lái ē seō, hā̤u-lái ē náng iā bā̤ gi̍ lē.
12 Guâ Ko̤-hi-le̍h, beh-céng ceo̍ Î-seh-le̍h ē ó̤ng, go̤h-do ceo̍ leo̍h Á̤-lō-sah-lêng.
13 Guâ ce̤ng-sing ē̤ng di̍-hā̤ gé̤ng-giu̍ ca-míng ting-ō̤ sê̤ ceo̍ ē seō, cuh ā̤ cai ca̤̍ sī ló̤-kô ē do̤̍ⁿ, Siō̤ng-Da̤̍ seo̍ keoh náng, a̍i-dí náng ing ah-na ga̍i ló̤-le̤̍h.
14 Guâ kua̍ⁿ ting-ō̤ sê̤ ceo̍ ē seō, deo sī se̤̍h leo̍h hṳ-kang, chā̤uⁿ kṳ̍ dia̍h boiⁿ seo̍h-ā̤uⁿ.
15 Uang-ke̤h ē, beó-dang-dng ba̤̍h ceo̍ di̍h; pā-kī ē, beó-dang-dng bô heô dôiⁿ.
16 Guâ sing lē ga̍i siô̤ng, gô̤ng, Guâ gi̍-céng deh duā ē gi-gia̍h, buh deh di̍-hā̤, bî guâ î-céng deó Á̤-lō-sah-lêng ē ce̤̍ng-náng ho̤h-ā̤ sā̤, guâ ē sing gṳ-do̤ ū lē̤ng-se̤̍h di̍-hā̤ gah di-sih.
17 N̄g-cṳ́ ce̤ng-sing kô̤-chah, seh-na ga̤̍u di̍-hā̤, seh-na ga̤̍u nguáng-dai, seh-na ga̤̍u gṳ́-cheông: î-hā̤u ā̤ cai ca̤̍ iā chā̤uⁿ kṳ̍ dia̍h boiⁿ seo̍h-ā̤uⁿ.
18 Ing di̍-hā̤ sā̤, huáng-nô̤ iā sā̤; di-sih ga-ting, iu-kô iā ga-ting.
## 2
1 Guâ sing lē ga̍i siô̤ng, gô̤ng, N̄g-cṳ́ ia̍ⁿ-ia̍ⁿ hî-lo̤̍h, meô hiô̤ng ang-háng; î-hā̤u cuh cai ca̤̍ iā sī hṳ-kang.
2 Guâ gô̤ng lāng-sâ̤u heô-cha̤̍u, Sī gó̤ng: hî-lo̤̍h ū seo̍h-moi iā lē?
3 Guâ sing lē buh ga̍i siô̤ng, guâ beoh kṳ̍ sia̍h ciû, sâi guā-tâ̤ sô̤ng-kua̍i, sing-lî cíng-céng seóng-cé̤ng di̍-hā̤, buh a̍i-dí bô̤-siû gṳ́-cheông, dêng ga̍u guâ ā̤ kua̍ⁿ-gi̍ng náng deó  ting-ō̤, seo̍h-sa̤̍-náng ceo̍ seo̍h-moi seō da̍i  heô.
4 Bíng ah-na, guâ cuh hing-kî duā ē gang-hu; kî ge̤ng-dēng, cāi bú-leó-hóiⁿ;
5 seh hóiⁿ ta̍h, cāi go̤h-cê̤ng ē go̍ih-cî-chiu̍ leo̍h do̤-ng;
6 guâ kui cûi-dí, ā̤ o̤h chiu̍-nó̤ lī-li̍ sê̤ saⁿ ē chiu̍;
7 guâ bâ̤ nó-bī, iā ū saⁿ leo̍h guâ cho̍-lî ē nó-bī; buh ṳ̂ hiô̤-sā̤ gú á̤uⁿ, bî guâ î-sá̤ⁿ diō̤ Á̤-lō-sah-lêng ē náng sê̤ ū ē ho̤h-ā̤ sā̤;
8 guâ buh có̤ng-cih ging-ngṳ́ng, léng go̤h-go̤h le̍h-ó̤ng sê̤ ting-dē̤ng beô-bo̍i ē mo̍ih-nō̤; buh deh cha̤̍uⁿ-geo ē náng-dṳ̂, léng sa̤̍-gang náng sê̤ tang-bō ē, gah hiô̤-sā̤ hó̤ng-ge̤ng ē hi.
9 Bíng ah-na, guâ duā hing-ō̤ng, dah hāng deo ga-ting, bî guâ î-céng diō̤ Á̤-lō-sah-lêng ē náng ga-ting ho̤h-ā sā̤: nō̤ guâ ē di̍-hā̤ cíng-céng ho̤h-diō̤.
10 Háng guâ ma̍h-ciu sê̤ a̍i-dí ē, deo n̄g gi̍ng-cî i; ing guâ gi̍-céng ló̤-kô ceo̍ gang-hu, guâ sing-lî hiô̤ng ang-lo̤̍h iā sī ha̍h-gímuâca̤̍ sī guâ iú ih-cheh ló̤-kô sê̤ deh ē heōng-nō̤.
11 Î-hā̤u guâ cuh kua̍ⁿ guâ chiû sê̤ héng ih-cheh ē seō, léng guâ sê̤ ló̤-kô deh da̤u̍h ē, cuh ā̤ cai deo sī hṳ-kang, chā̤uⁿ kṳ̍ dia̍h boiⁿ, diō̤ ting-ō̤ deo beó lī-ih.
12 Guâ sing-lî cuh dôiⁿ siô̤ng di̍-hā̤, gó̤ng-mō̤ng, gṳ́-cheông: ing guâ gi̍-céng ceo̍ ó̤ng, î-hā̤u ciah go̤h-ūi ē náng ā̤ ceo̍ seo̍h-moi lē? beoh-go̤̍ ce-i beh-náng sê̤ ceo̍ lâ̤u ē cuh sī.
13 Guâ cuh gi̍ng-gah di̍-hā̤ ā̤ ió̤ⁿ-geo̍ gṳ́-cheông, chā̤uⁿ gng-eng ió̤ⁿ-geo̍ o-a̍ng seo̍h-ā̤uⁿ.
14 Di̍-hā̤ ē náng háng seō ma̍h-ciu cing; gṳ́-cheông ē náng giáⁿ leo̍h o-a̍ng: nō̤ guâ gi̍ng-gah di̍-hā̤ gah ngō̤ng ē náng sê̤ gṳ̄-da̤u̍h ē seō deo sī seo̍h-ā̤uⁿ.
15 Guâ sing lē cuh siô̤ng, gô̤ng, Guâ sê̤ gṳ̄-da̤u̍h ē, gi̍-céng gah gṳ́-cheông ē náng sê̤ gṳ̄-da̤u̍h ē deo seo̍h-ā̤uⁿ, guâ deoh-nâi bî beh-náng ho̤h-ā̤ di̍-hā̤? Guâ sing lē cuh siô̤ng, gô̤ng, Ca̤̍ iā sī hṳ-kang. 
16 Di̍-hā̤ ē náng gah ngō̤ng náng seo̍h-ā̤uⁿ, ta̍u-dâ̤ deo beó náng gi̍-niāng i; ing hēng-cāi sê̤ ū ē, hā̤u-lái dih-dih deo beoh-gi̍-dih kṳ̍. Kô̤-sih di̍-hā̤ ē náng sî kṳ̍ gah ngō̤ng-náng beó geo̍-ā̤uⁿ.
17 Guâ hiáng ua̍h-miā; ing guâ gi̍ng ting-ō̤ sê̤ héng ē seō deo n̄g heô, deo sī hṳ-kang, chā̤uⁿ kṳ̍ dia̍h boiⁿ.
18 Guâ iā ga̍i hṳ̄ng deó ting-ō̤ ló̤-kô dâ-ti̍ng; ing guâ sê̤ deh ē, dih-dih láu keoh deō-bôi guâ lí ē náng sâi-ē̤ng.
19 I he̤̍ náng he̍h di̍-hā̤ he̍h ngō̤ng, hn̍g-náng ā̤ cai lē? Nō̤ guâ leo̍h ting-ō̤, ē̤ng di̍-hā̤ ló̤-kô sê̤ deh ē, dih-dih siū i guâng-lî; ca̤̍ iā sī hṳ-kang.
20 Ing-cheô guâ siô̤ng ga̍u guâ deó ting-ō̤ ló̤-kô dâ-ti̍ng ē seō, guâ ē sing cuh sih-mō̤ng.
21 Ing ū náng ē̤ng di̍-hā̤, di-sih, to̤ng-da̍h, gah ló̤-kô dâ-ti̍ng, cio̤ng ca̤̍ sê̤ deh lí ē, láu keoh beh-léng ló̤-kô ē náng ceo̍ gi-gia̍h; ca̤̍ iā sī hṳ-kang, cīng n̄g heô ē seō.
22 Náng leo̍h ting-ō̤ ló̤-le̤̍h, hi̍ cīng sing-gi, ga̍u-bôi ū seo̍h-moi deh lē?
23 I ta̍u-di̍h iu-chá̤u, dah-hāng seō-lâ̤ huáng-nô̤, i ē sing táu-má beó bíng-ang, ca̤̍ iā sī hṳ-kang.
24 Náng gi̍-céng leo̍h ló̤-kô do̤-ng, hó̤-beoh-cṳ́ meô sia̍h meô chōi, sâi i ē sing ang-lo̤̍h; guâ gi̍ng-gah ca̤̍ iā sī iú Siō̤ng-Da̤̍ ē chiû.
25 Nō̤ leōng ga̍u sê̤ sia̍h sê̤ hiô̤ng ē, hn̍g-náng ā̤ ió̤ⁿ-geo̍ guâ lē?
26 Siō̤ng-Da̤̍ sê̤ huaⁿ-hî ē náng, cuh ta̤̍h di̍-hā̤, di-sih, hî-lo̤̍h seo̍ keoh i; nō̤ deh-cōi leo̍h Siō̤ng-Da̤̍ ē náng, Siō̤ng-Da̤̍ cuh sâi i dng ca̤̍ siu-cih hiô̤-sā̤ mo̍ih-nō̤ ē ceh-cīng, î-di̍ gui keoh Siō̤ng-Da̤̍ sê̤ huaⁿ-hî ē náng sâi-ē̤ng. Ca̤̍ iā sī hṳ-kang, chā̤uⁿ kṳ̍ dia̍h boiⁿ.
## 3
1 Ting-ō̤ ih-cheh ē seō deo ū diāⁿ-da̤u̍h ē sí-cā̤, māng seō deo ū ā di̍h-gí:
2 saⁿ ū sí-cā̤, sî iā ū sí-cā̤; cai-ce̤̍ng ū sí-cā̤, ma̤̍h sê̤ cai ē iā ū sí-cā̤;
3 tái sio̤ng ū sí-cā̤, i-dī iā ū sí-cā̤; tiā ū sí-cā̤, kî iā ū sí-cā̤;
4 hâu ū sí-cā̤, cha̤̍u iā ū sí-cā̤; bi-sio̤ng ū sí-cā̤, ta̤̍u-bû iā ū sí-cā̤;
5 diāⁿ sa̤̍uh-táu ū sí-cā̤, kah sa̤̍uh-táu seo̍h-dui iā ū sí-cā̤; peō ū sí-cā̤, n̄g peō iā ū sí-cā̤;
6 cho̍i ū sí-cā̤, sih kṳ̍ iā ū sí-cā̤; ciû dua̍ ū sí-cā̤; ba̍ng kṳ̍ iā ū sí-cā̤;
7 tiā pua̍ ū sí-cā̤, bô ti̍ng iā ū sí-cā̤; beó ceo̍-siaⁿ ū sí-cā̤, gô̤ng-uā iā ū sí-cā̤;
8 sā̤u ū sí-cā̤, che̤ng iā ū sí-cā̤; gau-ce̍ng ū sí-cā̤, hó̤-hô̤ iā ū sí-cā̤.
9 Iú ciâ geh kua̍ⁿ seo̍h-gō̤, ceo̍ gang-hu ē náng ló̤-kô ū seo̍h-moi iā lē?
10 Guâ ū kua̍ⁿ-gi̍ng sa̤̍-gang náng ging-le̍h ló̤-le̤̍h, ā̤ cāi sī Siō̤ng-Da̤̍ sê̤ diāⁿ ē.
11 Siō̤ng-Da̤̍ cho̤̍ng-cho̤̍ māng-beo̍h, deo ū ha̍h sí-cā̤, beó ā n̄g-sī bā̤u-li̍ heô, buh sâi náng ē sing ā̤ chu̍i-do̤̍h sa̤̍-seō; nō̤ náng beó-dang-dng ching cai Siō̤ng-Da̤̍ iú táu ga̍u bôi ē sê̤ ceo̍ ē seō.
12 Guâ cai náng seo̍h-sa̤̍-náng, bí-do̤̍h hî-lo̤̍h gah héng-sēng sua̍ⁿ sī da̍i heô.
13 Go̤h-náng ā̤ sia̍h ā̤ chōi, ā̤ hiô̤ng ló̤-kô̤ sê̤ deh ē, ca̤̍ iā sī Siō̤ng-Da̤̍ sê̤ seo̍ ē.
14 Guâ cai Siō̤ng-Da̤̍ sê̤ ceo̍ ē dih-dih ṳ̂ng-ê̤ng deo diō̤, náng beó-dang-dng ga-ting, iā beó-dang-dng giâng-câ̤u; Siō̤ng-Da̤̍ ah-na ceo̍. a̍i-dí sâi náng gi̍ng-u̍i I.
15 Da sê̤ ū ē, î-cô̤ ū lâ̤u; cio̤ng-lái sê̤ beoh ū ē, î-céng ū lâ̤u: Siō̤ng-Da̤̍ iā sâi î-ô̤ng ē seō ca̍i ū.
16 Guâ buh gi̍ng ting-ō̤ sîng-pua̍ng ē ūi-chṳ̍, hn̄g-lî ū peng-seo; ing-gai héng-gī ē ūi-chṳ̍, huâng ū beoh-gī.

# Sê̤-ló̤-meóng Ē Ngâ-go̤

# Î-se̤̍-a̍ Cṳ

# Á̤-lī-bî Cṳ

# Á̤-lī-bî Ai-go̤ Cṳ

# Î-sa̤-geh Cṳ

# Dāng-î-lî Cṳ

# Hó̤-sa̤ Cṳ

# Io̤h-cî Cṳ

# A̍-mó̤-seo Cṳ

# O̤-ba-dâ̤ Cṳ

# Io̤h-ná Cṳ

# Bî-gia Cṳ

# Ná-o̤ng Cṳ

# Ha̍h-ba-go̤h Cṳ

# Sa̤-huang-ngâ Cṳ

# Ha̍h-gi Cṳ

# Sah-ga-lī-a̍ Cṳ

# Mâ-la̍h-gi Cṳ

# Mâ-ta̍i
## 1
1 A̍-beh-la̍h-hâng hā̤u-a̤̍, Dāi-bi̍h hā̤u-a̤̍, Á̤-So Gi-Do̤h ē co̤̍h-pô.
2 A̍-beh-la̍h-hâng saⁿ Î-sah, Î-sah saⁿ Ngâ-go̤h; Ngâ-go̤h saⁿ Iú-dāi, gah Iú-dāi hiaⁿ-dī.
3 Iú-dāi gah Dāi-mâ sī, saⁿ Hah-leh-seō, Sah-la̍h; Hah-leh-seō saⁿ Î-seō-leóng, Î-seō-leóng saⁿ A̍-láng;
4 A̍-láng saⁿ A̍-bî-ná-da̍h; A̍-bî-ná-da̍h saⁿ Ná-seōng; Ná-seōng saⁿ Sah-meóng.
5 Sah-meóng teô La̍h-ha̍h sī, saⁿ Bo̤-seō; Bo̤-seō teô Lō-deh sī, saⁿ O̤-beh; O̤-beh saⁿ Á-sa̤;
6 Á-sa̤ saⁿ Dāi-bi̍h ó̤ng; Dāi-bi̍h ó̤ng teô O-lī-a̍ ē lāu-mâ, saⁿ Sê̤-ló̤-meóng;
7 Sê̤-ló̤-meóng saⁿ Ló̤-bo̤-a̍ng; Ló̤-bo̤-a̍ng saⁿ A̍-bî-a̍; A̍-bî-a̍ saⁿ A̍-sah;
8 A̍-sah saⁿ Io̤h-sa-hah; Io̤h-sa-hah saⁿ Io̤h-láng; Io̤h-láng saⁿ O-sa̤-a̍;
9 O-sa̤-a̍ saⁿ Io̤h-da̍ng; Io̤h-da̍ng saⁿ A̍-ha̍h-seō; A̍-ha̍h-seō saⁿ Hi-sa̤-ga;
10 Hi-sa̤-ga saⁿ Mâ-ná-sa̤; Mâ-ná-sa̤ saⁿ A̍-meóng; A̍-meóng saⁿ Io̤h-sa̤-a̍.
11 Io̤h-sa̤-a̍ saⁿ Á̤-go̤-ní-a̍, léng Á̤-go̤-ní-a̍ hiaⁿ-dī. Hṳ̂ seo̍h sí bā-sa̍ⁿ buaⁿ kṳ̍ Ba-bî-leóng.
12 Buaⁿ Ba-bî-leóng āu, Á̤-go̤-ní-a̍ saⁿ Sah-la̍h-teh; Sah-la̍h-teh saⁿ Sê̤-ló̤-ba-beh;
13 Sê̤-ló̤-ba-beh saⁿ A̍-bî-eoh; A̍-bî-eoh saⁿ Î-lī-a̍-ging; Î-lī-a̍-ging saⁿ A̍-sê̤;
14 A̍-sê̤ saⁿ Sah-do̤̍h; Sah-do̤̍h saⁿ A̍-ging; A̍-ging saⁿ Î-leo̍h;
15 Î-leo̍h saⁿ Î-lī-a̍-sah; Î-lī-a̍-sah saⁿ Mâ-da̍ng; Mâ-da̍ng saⁿ Ngâ-go̤h;
16 Ngâ-go̤h saⁿ Io̤h-seh, cuh sī Mâ-lī-a̍ ē dah-bo; Mâ-lī-a̍ saⁿ Á̤-So, ching Gi-Do̤h.
17 Cé̤ng A̍-beh-la̍h-hâng ga̍u Dāi-bi̍h, se̍h-si̍ dāi; cé̤ng Dāi-bi̍h ga̍u buaⁿ Ba-bî-leóng sí-cā̤, se̍h-si̍ dāi, cé̤ng buaⁿ Ba-bî-leóng sí-cā̤ gá̤u Gi-Do̤h, se̍h-si̍ dāi.
18 Á̤-So Gi-Do̤h ē ga̍ng-seng, i ē seō-lâ̤ chā̤uⁿ ō̤-dâ̤. Ná̤u-lâ̤ Mâ-lī-a̍ keoh Io̤h-seh pi̍ng kṳ̍, bōi siáⁿ-náng, gâng-dō̤ng Si̍ng-Síng, cuh ū sing-ngî.
19 I dah-bo Io̤h-seh sī cíng-gī ē náng, n̄g a̍i hêng-hēng líng-ce̤̍h i, ī-ceóng seo-o̤ n̄g-dí i.
20 Cia̍ⁿ pā-sua̍ⁿ ca̤̍ seō sí-cā̤, Cṳ̂ ē teng-seo̍ deó ma̍ng de̤ng-gang hêng-hēng gah i gô̤ng, Dāi-bi̍h ē giô̤ⁿ-seong Io̤h-seh ā, da̤u̍h teô dṳ̂ lāu-mâ Mâ-lī-a̍ a̍ kṳ̍-cho̍, n̄g-sâi do̤-gí giaⁿ, ing i sê̤ dua̍-sing ē, sī iú Si̍ng-Síng.
21 I beóh saⁿ giô̤ⁿ, da̤u̍h heō miá I ga̤̍u Á̤-So, ing beoh giu̍ I bā-sa̍ⁿ leo̍h cōi-o̤h do̤-ng.
22 Háng ca̤̍ seō deh siáⁿ, si i̍ng-ngiāng Cṳ̂ to̤h seng-di sê̤ gô̤ng ē uā, gô̤ng,
23 Sîng-ná̤u-giô̤ⁿ beoh dua̍-sing saⁿ giô̤ⁿ, náng beóh ching I miá Î-mâ-dōi-lī, huang-i̍h cuh sī Siō̤ng-Da̤̍ gah guâ-go̤h-náng seo̍h-dui.
24 Io̤h-seh châⁿ, cuh bíng Cṳ̂ ē teng-seo̍ sê̤ heong-nu̍ ē uā kṳ̍ ceo̍, teô i lāu-mâ geo̍-mói.
25 Bí-do̤̍h bōi gah i ceo̍ seo̍h báng-ga̤ⁿ, ga̍u a̤u ā guā-giô̤ⁿ; cuh miá I ga̤̍u Á̤-So.

## 2
1 Dng Hi-leo̍h Hi-leo̍h ó̤ng sí-cā̤, Á̤-So saⁿ leo̍h Iú-ta̍i Beh-lī-héng, ū bo̤h-seō gûi gá̤, cé̤ng dang-ha̤̍uⁿ ga̍u Á̤-lō-sah-lêng,
2 ka̍ng gô̤ng, No̤̍ saⁿ Iú-ta̍i náng ē ó̤ng deó deoh-leo? guâ-go̤h-náng deó dang-ha̤̍uⁿ kua̍ⁿ-gi̍ng I ē chaⁿ; go̍-cheô lí ba̍i I.
3 Hi-leo̍h ó̤ng tiaⁿ-mói cuh giaⁿ-peo̍h, to̤ng Á̤-lō-sah-lêng iā ah-na.
4 Cuh dā̤u ce̤̍ng ca̤̍-si-diô̤ng, gá̤h míng-gang ta̍h-cṳ-náng, ka̍ng i gô̤ng, Gi-Do̤h ing-gai saⁿ leo̍h seo̍h-moi ūi-chṳ̍?
5 Go̤h-náng i̍ng gô̤ng, Leo̍h Iú-ta̍i ē Beh-lī-héng; i̍ng seng-di ū ah-na gi̍-ca̍i gô̤ng,
6 Iú-ta̍i dā Beh-lī-héng ā! dṳ̂ deó Iú-ta̍i hû gā̤ⁿ do̤-ng, n̄g-sī da̍i sa̤̍ ē, ing-ūi beoh ū go̤h-geong iú dṳ̂ hiô̤ do̍ung cheoh, ta̤̍h n̍g guâ Î-seh-le̍h bā-sa̍ⁿ.
7 Iú ah-na, Hi-leo̍h ó̤ng cuh bōi-āu dā̤u bo̤h-seō, do̤ng-sió̤ng ka̍ng I chaⁿ cheoh ē sí-cā̤.
